{{left

#### Alignment
You gain a level of Lunar Alignment.

#### Endurance
You start with 16 *Endurance* and gain 6 per level.

#### Training

**Actions** :: You can take Arcane Actions.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[INT] Save Checks in Tandem with your Arcana Die, and they are Empowered.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[INT] and Arcana Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells** :: Your Arcana Die is your Spell Mod Die. Checks you make using your Spell Mod Die count as $[INT] Checks.

}}

### Arcane Triumvirate < lvl 1 >
Select your Arcane Triumvirate from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Triumvirate is the organization you studied under to become an Arcanist. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Prismacasting < lvl 1 >

You can cast spells by tapping into the ambient light that suffuses the facets, known to Arcanists as ***Prisma***.

#### Arcanist's Grimoire
You have authored a book containing five 1st-level arcanum spells, known as your **Grimoire**. The spells you can add to your Grimoire are restricted by your Arcane Triumvirate.

#### Recording Spells
While you have a written copy of an arcanum, such as in a book or scroll, you can copy the spell into your Grimoire. Doing so takes 4 hours per level of the spell, or half that if its type is taught by your Triumvirate.

#### Replacing your Grimoire
You can spend one week of Downtime to create a copy of your Grimoire. If you Grimoire is ever destroyed, you can create a new one over a Long Rest, recording the spells you had prepared that day into the new one.

\column

#### Preparing Spells
Each Long Rest, you can **Prepare** a number of arcana from your Grimoire equal to your Arcanist level plus your $[INT] points, allowing you to cast those spells until your next Long Rest. Spells prepared this way must have a level no higher than your Max Spell Level, as shown in the Arcanist Class Table.

#### Casting Spells
Arcanists gather and spend Prisma to cast spells. The total amount of Prisma you can spend each Long Rest is listed in the Prisma Limit column on the Arcanist Class Table. Each time you finish a Short Rest, the amount of Prisma applied to your Prisma Limit is reduced by your Arcanist Bonus.

You gather Prisma using the **Refract** Action, and you cast spells with the **Primacast** Action, each described below.

##### Refract
{{left
**Categories** :: Arcane, Move, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: 1 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: You have less *Prisma* than your Max Spell Level; your Prisma Limit hasn’t been reached and wouldn’t be reached by spending your current *Prisma*
}}
You gain 1 *Prisma*. Unspent *Prisma* you have gathered dissipates at the end of your next turn if you don’t Refract on that turn.

##### Prismacast
**Categories** :: Arcane, Move, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: $[SL] *Prisma*, $[AP] in spell’s Cast Time

You cast one of your cantrips or Prepared spells at a level up to your Max Spell Level. You then become Prismaburned $[SL]. Each time you Sustain the spell, you regain that Prismaburned level.

###### Surecasting
If your Grimoire is open in front of you and you can read it, you can instead spend an additional 2$[AP] to Surecast any spell recorded in it, even if you don’t have it Prepared. Surecasting only requires one hand if the spell would require two. You can Interact with your Grimoire to open it while you have a free hand.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Prismaburn
When you Prismacast or Sustain a Prismacast spell, you become Prismaburned $[SL]. The Condition is described below.

##### Prismaburned X
When you attempt to Prismacast a non-Reaction spell, your Max Spell Level is reduced by X.

The level of this Condition reduces by 1 at the start of your turn.

### Prismatic Scholar < lvl 1 >
As an accomplished scholar of the arcane, you have become sensitive to the presence of concentrated prisma. When you Identify Arcane, you can replace your $[PER] Die with your Arcana Die. You also gain the **Detect Arcane** Action, described below.

##### Detect Arcane
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 1 *Prisma*, 2 $[AP]

You become aware of arcane effects within your $[PER] Radius for 10 minutes. During that time, objects, areas, and creatures with arcane effects glow faintly to you, and you can spend 2 $[AP] to Identify Arcane entities as long as they are within your $[PER] radius. You cannot Detect Arcane through 1 inch of metal, 1 foot of stone, or 3 feet of wood or dirt.

### Prismatic Realignment < lvl 2 >

As an adept prismacaster, you have a higher capacity for handling Prisma. You gain the **Realign** action, described below.
:
##### Realign

**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: You haven’t Realigned since your last Long Rest

You remove all *Prisma* applied to your Prisma Limit, and your Prismaburn level is reduced by half your Arcanist Bonus.

### Arcane Flow < lvl 2 >
Where others see domain and hierarchy, Arcanists see synergy. After Prismacasting a spell, any Checks you make for the next spell you Prismacast before the end of your next turn are rolled in Tandem with your $[INT] Die if both spells were of different types.

Any Checks you make as part of Surecasting a spell are also rolled in Tandem with your $[INT] Die.

<!--### Arcane Scars < lvl 3 >
Your arcane experimentation has left you permanently prismaburned, forever marking you as an arcanist. The veins on your hands now glow lightly with the colors of Prisma used by your Triumvirate.

When your Max Spell Level increases, you prismaburns deepen, as described below.
:
**3rd** Your prismaburns now glow vibrantly with the color of prisma you use most.
:
**4th** Faint prismaburns have developed in the veins around your eyes, a scar earned by only the most experienced arcanists.
:
**5th** A paragon of arcane talent, your prismaburns now reach all the way up your arms and across your face, glowing bright against your skin. 

### Arcane Discovery < lvl 4 >

You make an emergent arcane discovery, allowing you to record one arcanum of any type into your Grimoire. The arcanum must be of a level equal to or lower than your Max Spell Level.

You make an additional Arcane Discovery at 7th and 10th level.

### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >

You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 7th level.

### Signature Arcanum < lvl 5 >

Choose an arcanum recorded in your Grimoire of a level at least 2 below your Max Spell Level. That arcanum becomes your **Signature Arcanum**, which you can alter in one of the following ways each time you Prismacast it:

- You can reduce the Casting Time by 1 $[AP] (minimum 1 $[AP]) if you haven't yet chosen this option for the Signature Arcana you are Prismacasting on the current turn.

- You can roll your $[INT] Die in Tandem with your Arcana Die for any Checks made for that casting.

- If you Surecast it, you can spend 1 additional *Prisma* to Empower one Check made for that casting.

\pag

Each time you gain an Arcanist level, you can swap your chosen Signature Arcanum.

### Expert Prismacaster < lvl 6 >

Now an erudite spellcrafter, the Sustain action costs 1 less $[AP] for you to take if you are Sustaining an arcanum.

Your Prismaburn level also reduces by 2 at the end of your turn, instead of 1.

### Archprism < lvl 8 >

A true master of the arcane, you gain an additional Signature Arcanum as described in that feature, and your Signature Arcana cost 1 less *Prisma* to Prismacast (minimum 1 *Prisma*).

Additionally, you can take the Realign action twice per Long Rest.

### Paragon Facet  < lvl 10 >

You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.-->

\column

## Arcane Triumvirates

Each Arcanist belongs to or once studied under a Triumvirate, a collection of like-minded Arcanists that specialize in the same three prisma types. The teachings of an Arcanist's Triumvirate shape their approach to prismacasting.

### Kinesis Triumvirate

Specializing in Ice, Fire, and Arc prisma, Arcanists of the Kinesis Triumvirate are masters of manipulating the energy that flows through physical matter. They are among the most destructively potent Arcanists.

#### Kinetic Specialization < lvl 1 >
As a student of the Kinesis Triumvirate, the spells you discover follow the rules below. **Kinesis Arcana** are those of the **Fire**, **Ice**, and **Arc** type.
:
- ***Initial Arcana*** You must choose at least one Kinesis Arcanum of each type for the initial five spells recorded in your Grimoire.

- ***Initial Cantrips*** You learn three cantrips, two of which must be Kinesis Arcana.

- ***Level Up*** When you gain an Arcanist level, you record two arcana into your Grimoire of a level up to your Max Spell Level, one of which must be a Kinesis Arcanum.

- ***Bonus Spells*** At 4th, 7th, and 10th level, you learn an additional cantrip of any type, and you record an additional arcanum of any type into your Grimoire of a level up to your Max Spell Level.

#### Kinetic Font < lvl 1 >
You can Prismacast any Kinesis Arcanum recorded in your Grimoire without spending any *Prisma*. The spell must have a level no higher than half your Arcanist Bonus, but you don’t need to have it Prepared.

Once you use this facet, you must finish a Long Rest before you can use it again.

#### Potential Conversion  < lvl 1 >
When you Prismacast a Kinesis cantrip that involves an Attack or DC Check, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP]. If you do, the Check is Bolstered 1.

You also gain a level of Flame, Frost, and Velvet Alignment.

<!--#### Kinetic Manipulation < lvl 4 >

When you cast an arcanum that deals arctic, thermic, or galvanic damage, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] to swap that damage type for another of those damage types.

You can also use Kinetic Font once per Short Rest, rather than once per Long Rest.

#### Master Kineticist < lvl 7 >

When you deal arctic, thermic, or galvanic damage with an Attack Check for an arcanum, you ignore resistance to those damage types equal to your Arcanist Bonus. Also, each time you cast an Ice, Fire, or Arc arcanum that affects an area, you can modify the spell in one of the following ways:

- You can increase the size of the area by 1 space (such as increasing a 3-space cone to a 4-space cone), and any Allies in the affected area of the spell automatically score a Complete Success on any Checks you choose for that spell. 

- You can reduce the size of the area by 1 space. If you do, any Check you make for that spell is Bolstered 1.

If the area has more than one dimension, you change only one of them.

#### Cataclysmic Font < lvl 10 >

Your spellcraft beckons a cataclysm. Once per long rest when you use Kinetic Font, rather than casting a single spell, you can cast one Ice, one Fire, and one Arc arcanum with combined levels equal to your Arcanist Bonus (cantrips count as level 0 arcana). The $[AP] required to cast these spells is combined, but if it exceeds 5, you instead only need to spend 5 $[AP]-->

![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center
## Berserker
Whirlwinds of impassioned, fervorous rage, none can ignore the Berserker's cry as they trample across the Prism.
}}

{{classTable,wide
##### Berserker Class Table
|       | Berserker |                                                   | Wild    |         | Maneuvers | Max Maneuver |
| Level | Bonus     | Facets                                            | Die     | Stamina | Known     | Level        |
|:-----:|:---------:|:--------------------------------------------------|:-------:|:-------:|:---------:|:------------:|
|  1st  |    +2     | Wild Fervor, Berserker Maneuvers, Barbarous Light |   1d6   |    4    |     2     |      1st     |
|  2nd  |    +2     | Reckless Assault, Somatic Wellspring              |   1d6   |    5    |     3     |      1st     |
}}

{{left

#### Alignment
You gain a level of Soma Alignment.

#### Endurance
You start with 26 *Endurance* and gain 16 per level.

#### Training

**Actions** :: You can take Combat Actions.

**Armor** :: You can add your Berserker level to your AC while not wearing armor.

**Maneuvers** :: Your Wild Die is your Maneuver Mod Die.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[VIG], $[PER], and $[AGI] Save Checks in Tandem with your Wild Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[VIG] and Wild Die in tandem, it is Empowered.

**Weapons** :: You can wield any Heavy or Massive melee weapon.

}}

### Wild Fervor < lvl 1 >
Select your Wild Fervor from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Wild Fervor represents the intense emotion through which you channel your strength. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Berserker Maneuvers < lvl 1 >
Your rabid ferocity allows you to execute **Feral** and **Instinctual** maneuvers by spending ***Stamina***. You start with a maximum of 4 *Stamina*, and your maximum increases each level, as shown in the Stamina column of the Berserker Class Table. When you execute a maneuver, you must spend *Stamina* equal to the maneuver’s level. You regain spent *Stamina* when you finish a Rest.

You know one 1st-level maneuver of your choice from the types you can learn (Feral and Instinctual) and the **Rage** maneuver, described below. You learn an additional maneuver each level, as shown in the Maneuvers Known column of the Berserker Class Table. The maximum maneuver level you’re allowed to learn is shown in the Max Maneuver Level column on that table as well.

\column

#### Rage
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Requirements** :: You can sense a Hostile creature
**Duration** :: Sustained (1 $[AP]), 10 minutes

Your ferocity is so intense, it siphons Soma Light into your body. For the duration, you can’t cast spells or Sustain other effects, and you gain the benefits below.
:
- ***$[VIG] Checks.*** Your $[VIG] Checks are Bolstered $[SL].
  
- ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* increases by $[SL].
  
- ***Bulwark.*** Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing damage you take is halved.
  
- ***Resistance.*** You have 2$[SL] Resistance to Arctic, Caustic, Galvanic, Somatic, and Thermic damage.

You can’t spend additional *Stamina* to execute this Maneuver at a higher level as normal. Instead, each time you Sustain this maneuver you may spend 1 *Stamina* to increase its level by 1, up to your Max Maneuver Level.

### Barbarous Light < lvl 1 >
Your body is an instrument of destruction, granting you the benefits below.
:
- ***Unarmed Strikes.*** When you Unarmed Strike, your body has the Pushing property, its DV equals your $[VIG] Points, and its Deadly DV equals twice that amount.

- ***Powerful Jump.*** The height of your High Jump equals 1 plus your $[VIG] points, rather than half your $[VIG] points, and the maximum distance of your Long Jump after moving equals twice your $[VIG] points, instead of 2 plus your $[VIG] points, if it would be larger.

- ***Wild Instinct.*** Your Initiative Checks are rolled in Tandem with your Wild Die.

- ***Heavy Weapons.*** Heavy melee weapons don’t have the Two-Handed property while you wield them.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Reckless Assault < lvl 2 >
During your turn, you can choose to recklessly charge your enemies until the start of your next turn. Doing so grants the effects below.
:
- ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* increases by 2

- ***Power.*** Melee Attack Checks you make are rolled in Tandem with your Wild Die.

- ***Reckless.*** Attack Checks made against you have Advantage.

### Somatic Wellspring < lvl 2 >
You are imbued with a near-bottomless wellspring of Soma Light. Choose $[VIG], $[AGI], or $[PER] as the Attribute through which you channel this wellspring.

When you Rage or increase your Rage level, you can choose to unleash this wellspring. You gain a benefit based on your chosen attribute, listed below. Once you use this facet, you must finish a Rest before you can use it again.

At 5th and 8th level, you gain additional benefits based on your choice.
#### Vigor
You regain expended *Stamina* equal to your $[VIG] points.
#### Agility
For the duration of your Rage, your *Movement* is doubled.
#### Perception
For the duration of your Rage, you your AC gains a bonus equal to your $[PER] points.

\column

## Wild Fervors
Berserkers gain their strength from the depth and power of their emotions. Their Wild Fervor represents an emotion they feel so deeply, it evokes response from the Prismos itself, bolstering their bodies into forces of nature.

### Roaring Fervor
You are anger incarnate, and you wield your anger as a ravenous flail upon the battlefield. Berserkers of this fervor are figures of legend, leaving trails of bodies in the echo of their endless fury.

#### Roaring Rage < lvl 1 >
Some of this fervor’s facets require you to **Roar**, releasing a loud shout from the depths of your lungs. Your Rage gains the following additional benefits which require you to Roar.
:
- ***Adrenaline.*** After taking damage during Rage, you may Roar. If you do, you gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to your Berserker Bonus.

- ***Frenzy.*** When you make an Attack Check, you may Roar and sacrifice *Temporary Endurance* up to your Berserker level. On a Hit, the target takes additional damage of a type dealt by the attack equal to twice the amount sacrificed, or three times on a Deadly Hit.

- ***Fear.*** When you Roar, each Hostile creature within an emanation out to your Berserker Bonus is Numbed 1 until the end of your next turn.

#### Voice of the Feral < lvl 1 >
When you execute a maneuver, you may choose to Roar and roll your Wild Die. If the result is higher than your remaining Stamina, the maneuver's level increases by 1. Once you use this facet, you must finish a Rest before you can use it again.

#### Bellowing Might < lvl 1 >
When you make a $[VIG] Attribute Check, you may Roar to roll it in Tandem with your Wild Die, and you can choose to Rage before making the Check, ignoring the Requirements to do so.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center

## Champion

Fervent believers in a cause beyond themselves, Champions harbor a will so strong that light itself answers their call-to-arms.

}}

{{classTable,wide
##### Champion Class Table
|       | Champion  |                                            | Devotion |         | Exploits | Max Exploit |
| Level | Bonus     | Facets                                     | Die     | Will | Known     | Level        |
|:-----:|:---------:|:-------------------------------------------|:-------:|:-------:|:---------:|:------------:|
|  1st  |    +2     | Champion’s Cause, Champion’s Aura, Apostle’s Light  |   1d6   |    -    |     -     |      -     |
|  2nd  |    +2     | Glorious Exploits, Undaunted                 |   1d6   |    2    |     3     |      1st     |
<!--|  3rd  |    +3     | Evoke Ally                                    |   1d6   |    2    |     4     |      1st     |-->
}}

{{descriptive,text-align:left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Infernal Alignment

#### Endurance Points
You start with 24 *Endurance* and gain 14 per level.

#### Training

**Actions**      :: You can take Combat and Supernatural Actions.

**Armor**        :: You can add your Champion Bonus to your AC while wearing any kind of armor.

**Maneuvers**    :: Your Devotion Die is your Maneuver Mod Die.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[VIG] and $[PRS] Save Checks in Tandem with your Devotion Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PRS] and Devotion Die in tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Your Devotion Die is your Spell Mod Die. Checks you make using your Spell Mod Die count as $[PRS] Checks.

**Weapons**      :: You can wield any weapon.
}}

### Champion’s Cause < lvl 1 >
Select your Champion’s Cause from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Cause is the greater purpose you devote yourself towards that makes you a Champion. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 2nd, 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Champion’s Aura < lvl 1 >
Your presence radiates a power that others feel in their very soul. While awake, you have an **Aura** that extends from you in an emanation, the size of which equals your Champion Bonus. Choose your preferred approach to championing your cause from those listed at the end of the class description. You gain benefits based on your choice, and you gain additional benefits at 5th and 8th level.

Each time you gain a Champion level, you may swap the choice you made for your Champion’s Aura.

\column

### Apostle’s Light < lvl 1 >
The devotion you carry pushes you forward where others would fall. You gain additional *Vitality* equal to half your Champion bonus, and you roll your Devotion Die in Tandem with your $[VIG] Die for Survival Save Checks.

Also, when you roll a Tandem $[INT] / $[PRS] Check to persuade someone of anything relating to your Champion’s Cause, you can replace your $[INT] Die with your Devotion Die for that Check.

### Glorious Exploits < lvl 2 >
Your ***Will*** resonates so strongly, it bends the Prismos to your aid. You start with a maximum of 2 *Will*, which you can spend to execute certain Champion facets. Your maximum increases each level, as shown in the Will column of the Champion Class Table. You regain expended *Will* when you finish a Rest.

You can spend *Will* to execute **Glorious Exploits**, which are spells and maneuvers you learn at certain Champion levels as shown in the Exploits Known column of the Champion Class Table. The maximum Exploit level you’re allowed to learn is shown in the Max Exploit Level column on that table as well. The Exploits you can choose are determined by your Champion’s Cause.

#### Executing Maneuvers
To execute a Maneuver, you must expend *Will* equal to half its level.

#### Casting Spells
You gain the **Decree** action and you learn the **Smite** Evocation as your first Glorious Exploit, both described below.
:
##### Decree
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: $[SL] *Will*, $[AP] in spell’s Cast Time
**Requirements** :: You haven’t decreed this turn

You cast one of your mantras or Exploit spells at a level up to your Max Exploit Level. Evocations cast this way require no hands.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

##### Smite
*Evocation (type varies), 1st-level*

**Cast Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Components** :: One Hand or a Weapon
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You Strike.

Roll your Spell Mod Die in Tandem with your Attribute Die for the Attack Check of your Strike, which gains additional effects determined by your Champion’s Cause.

### Undaunted < lvl 2 >
Your mission comes before everything, including yourself. On your turn you can sacrifice 1 *Vitality* (no $[AP] required) to do one of the following:

:

- ***Press On.*** You regain *Endurance* equal to 10 times your Champion Bonus.

- ***Overcome.*** Perform a Glorious Exploit without spending any *Will* ($[AP] is spent as normal). The Exploit is performed at one level above your Max Exploit Level if it's a spell, or two above if it's a maneuver.

:

You don't begin Dying from sacrificing *Vitality* this way. Once you use this facet, you must finish a Rest before you can use it again.

<!--### Evoke Ally < lvl 3 >
The fervor of your call reaches to the very depths of the Prismos, pulling allies from other facets. You learn the **Evoke Ally** Evocation, described below.
:
##### Evoke Ally
*Evocation (type varies), 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]

This spell ends early if you cast it again. You can spend also end the spell on your turn by spending 1 $[AP], sending the ally back to its home facet.

Your Supernatural Ally uses the stat block below.

\column

##### Traits
**Summon** :: The ally fights under your command using the following rules.
:
* ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the ally, but you roll its Checks.

* ***$[AP] Maximum.*** The ally’s $[AP] Maximum is 3.

* ***Allied.*** It has a supernatural understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required) and will follow them to the best of its ability if they align with or progress your Champion’s Cause.

* ***Initiative.*** If summoned during an encounter, it shares your Initiative and takes its turn after yours.

:
**Resonance** :: When you Smite, the ally regains *Endurance* equal to the damage dealt.

**Knight (Combatant only)** :: The Combatant can take Combat Actions, but Standing costs 2 $[AP] for it.

**Steed (Mount only)** ::The Mount can’t take Manipulate Actions. You can mount or dismount the ally by spending 2 $[AP] as a Manipulate Move Action on your turn.
:

**Radiant Weapon (Combatant only; d8 Mod Die)** :: $[SL] plus 3 / $[SL] plus 6 (damage type varies), Heavy

### Champion’s Aura
Champions evoke power from the unwavering devotion to their cause, but each goes about it in a different way. Choose your preferred method of championing your cause from those below.

#### Connection
You believe in the power of connection and that only together can progress be achieved.

:

**1st Level** You can Assist any creature making an Attack Check within your Aura, and Assisting a creature within your Aura costs 1 less $[AP]. The assisted creature gains *Temporary Endurance* equal to twice your $[PRS] Points.
:
You also gain a level of Temporal Alignment.

<!--**5th Level** Allies within your Aura have Panicked resistance equal to half your Champion Bonus.

**8th Level** The DC of PRS Save Checks you and Allies within your Aura make is reduced by the number of Allies within your Aura (not counting yourself).-->

#### Force
You believe that absolute authority is the only way to bring change.

:

**1st Level** When you Strike a creature within your aura, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] to roll your $[PRS] Die in Tandem with your Attribute Die for the Attack Check.
:
You also gain a level of Infernal Alignment.

<!--**5th Level** Hostile creatures within your Aura are Numbed 1.

**8th Level** When you drop a creature within your Aura to 0 *Endurance* with an Attack Check, each Hostile creature within your Aura must make a PRS Save Check against the result of the Attack Check.

**Failure** Panicked 1
**Complete Failure** Panicked 2
**Success** nothing-->

\column

#### Subversion
You prefer to mislead your opposition and advance your cause in shadow, keeping your true beliefs hidden.

:

**1st Level** Your AC gets a bonus against Attack Checks made by creatures within your Aura equal to half your Champion Bonus. When a creature within your Aura forces you to make an $[AGI] Save Check, it’s rolled in Tandem with your Devotion Die.
:
You also gain a level of Lunar Alignment, and when you roll a Tandem $[INT] / $[PRS] Check to lie to someone, you can replace your $[INT] Die with your Devotion Die for that Check.

<!--**5th Level** Hostile Creatures within your Aura are Muddled 1.

**8th Level** When you spend *Light*, you can become Invisible to any creature within your Aura until the end of your turn.-->

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

## Champion's Causes
Be it for Justice, revenge, power or glory, a Champion's Cause is their strength, that which they would sacrifice everything for. A Champion's devotion to their cause is so potent, it moves the very World Prism to action.

### The Throne
For power, authority, and the rule of the strong. Champions of the throne demand that a greater will is brought forth, and that lesser wills bend the knee.

#### Infernal Will < lvl 1 >
Strengthened by your own might, when you Succeed on an Attack Check against a creature and when a creature suffers a Failure against your DC Check, you regain *Endurance* equal to half your Champion Bonus, or your Champion Bonus if the result was Deadly.

If multiple creatures make a Save Check against your DC Check, this facet triggers only for the creature with the highest result.

You also regain 1 spent *Radiance* when you roll a 19 on the d20 for an Attack Check.

#### Evoke Submission < lvl 1 >
You gain the **Evoke Submission** Action, described below.
:
##### Evoke Submission
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Hostile
**Cost** :: 1 *Radiance,* 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: Spoken

You emit an aura of authority. Each Hostile creature within your Aura makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your Champion DC Check.

:
{{left
**Failure** :: Prone, Immobilized until the end of the creature’s next turn.
**Complete Failure** :: As a Failure, and the creature repeats its Save Check against this effect (you make a new DC Check).
**Success** :: Prone.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
}}

Once you Evoke Submission, you must finish a Rest before you can do so again.

#### Throne Exploits < lvl 2 >
As a Champion of the Throne, you can learn **Martial** and **Psychological** Maneuvers, as well as **Flame** and **Infernal** Evocations when you gain a new Glorious Exploit. Choose two Exploits to learn when you gain this facet in addition to Smite.

\column

##### Dominating Smite
Your Smite gains the Infernal type, and when you Smite, your Attack Check gains the additional effects below.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 2 $[SL] Chthonic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 3 $[SL] Chthonic damage, Possessed $[SL].
**Failure** :: $[SL] Chthonic damage.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}
<!--### Darkness

In darkness, we are all equals. Champions of Darkness serve to push all things towards their natural end, and ward against the evils of immortality and prolonged existence. Everything must end, eventually.

#### Vital Sacrifice < lvl 1 >
You gain the **Evoke Darkness** action, described below.
:
##### Evoke Darkness
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Hostile
**Cost** :: 1 *Vitality* (you don’t begin Dying), 2 $[AP]; You haven’t Evoked Darkness since your last Rest.
}}

You briefly shroud yourself in darkness. Each Hostile creature within your Aura makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your Spell DC Check.

:

{{left
**Failure** :: Panicked 2.
**Complete Failure** :: Panicked 3.
**Success** :: Panicked 1.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Endwalker < lvl 1 >
The closer you are to the grave, the stronger you become. You contain **Darkness** equal to your missing *Vitality*, giving you the following benefits.

:

* ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* gains a bonus equal to your Darkness.

* ***Damage.*** Each time you take or deal damage, you can increase or reduce one type of the damage by half your Darkness.

#### Darkness Exploits < lvl 2 >
As a Champion of Darkness, you can learn Feral and Martial Maneuvers, as well as Void and Infernal Evocations when you gain a new Glorious Exploit. Choose two Exploits to learn when you gain this facet in addition to Smite.

##### Void Smite
Your Smite gains the Void type, and when you Smite, your Attack Check is against the target’s $[VIG] and gains the following additional effects.

:

{{left
**Success** :: $[SL] Divine damage, and the target loses 1 *Vitality*.
**Deadly Success** :: 2 $[SL] Divine damage, and the target loses 2 *Vitality*.
**Failure** :: The target loses *Endurance* equal to its Level.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

\column

#### Void Ally < lvl 3 >
Your Supernatural Ally has no creature type, and gains the following additional facets.

:
**Voidborn** The ally has Resistance to all damage types equal to your Darkness.
:
**Darkblade (Combatant only)** The ally’s Radiant Weapon deals Divine damage, and on a Deadly Success, the target loses *Endurance* equal to twice its Level.
:
**Shadowstep (Mount only)** Each time the ally Strides, it can also Teleport a number of spaces up to your Darkness. You Teleport with it if you are riding it.
:
**Shadowhaunt (Retainer only)** The ally’s Simple Weapon deals Divine damage, and it can merge with your shadow by Stepping into it, becoming intangible and undetectable except by Identifying Supernatural. It can Step out of your shadow on its turn.

#### Voidbringer < lvl 4 >
Each time you take or deal damage, you can increase or reduce one type of the damage by your full Darkness, instead of half.

When you Smite, your Strike creates a void rift, allowing you to target creatures a number of spaces away from you equal to your Darkness.

#### Harbinger of Death < lvl 7 >
WWhen you or a Hostile creature within your Aura loses *Vitality*, you gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to your Champion Level.

#### Dark Knight < lvl 10 >
When you use Undaunted, you become shrouded in the pitch black void and gain 2 temporary $[AP], all of which lasts until the end of your turn. While shrouded in this void, your Darkness is doubled.-->

## Expert

Jack of all trades and master of several, Experts leverage their extensive experience to great effect.

}}

{{descriptive
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Frost Alignment.

#### Endurance Points
You start with 20 *Endurance* and gain 10 per level.

#### Training

**Armor**        :: You can add your Expert Bonus to your AC while wearing Light or Medium armor.

**Maneuvers**    :: Your Tactical Die is your Maneuver Mod Die.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[PER] and $[INT] Save Checks in Tandem with your Tactical Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PER] and Tactical Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Weapons**      :: You can wield any Light, Close-Up, or Precise weapon.

### Expert Practice < lvl 1 >
Select your Expert Practice from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Practice is the career that gave you the majority of your skills. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Expert Maneuvers < lvl 1 >
Your varied skillset allows you to execute **Instinctual** and **Tactical** maneuvers by spending ***Stamina***. You start with a maximum of 3 *Stamina*, and your maximum increases each level, as shown in the Stamina column of the Expert Class Table. When you execute a maneuver, you must spend *Stamina* equal to the maneuver’s level. You regain expended *Stamina* when you finish a Rest.

Choose two 1st-level maneuvers of the types you can learn (Instinctual and Tactical) as your initial maneuvers. You learn an additional maneuver each level as shown in the Maneuvers Known column of the Expert Class Table. The maximum maneuver level you're allowed to learn is shown in the Max Maneuver Level column on that table as well.

\column

### Expertise < lvl 1 >
You’ve spent your life sharpening your skills to a fine edge, granting you the facets below.

#### Highly Skilled
Any Skill Check you make using the Mod Die of a Hobby or Profession is Bolstered with a level equal to half your Expert Bonus.

#### Additional Hobbies
You gain two additional Hobbies at Amateur Rank. You gain another Hobby at Amateur Rank time your Expert Bonus increases.

#### Expert's Tools
An Expert always has their tools on hand. Your **Expert’s Tools** allow you to make any Check involving one of your Hobbies or Professions that would normally require portable tools as if you had them.

Choose one type of Light weapon, one type of Close-Up weapon, and one type of Precise weapon. The chosen weapons become part of your Expert’s Tools. When you Strike, you can use a weapon of any type on your person designated as part of your Expert’s Tools without having to Interact to draw it or stow a different weapon.

You can change your chosen weapon types each time you gain a level.

#### Adroit Strikes
You can leverage your array of skills to attack with precision. Once per turn when you Strike a creature, you can replace the Mod Die of the Attack Check with your Tactical Die. To do so, one of the below conditions must be met.
:

- ***Advantage.*** You have Advantage on the Attack Check.

- ***Distracted.*** There is an Ally within 1 space of the target that has at least 1 $[AP] remaining and is not Unconscious.

- ***Focused.*** You spent at least 3 $[AP] on the Strike (not including CAP).

For that Attack Check, add your Expert Bonus to the DV of your weapon, and add twice that to the Deadly DV.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Evaluate < lvl 2 >
Using your vast experience, you can prepare unorthodox ways to overcome challenges. You gain the **Evaluate** reaction, described below.
:
##### Evaluate
{{left
**Categories**   :: Concentrate
**Cost**         :: Reaction, 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You have not taken a Move Action on the current turn.
**Trigger**      :: You make a Check.
}}

Choose one of your Hobbies or Professions. The triggering Check is rolled in Tandem with the Mod Die of the chosen Hobby or Profession.

You can Evaluate with each of your Hobbies once per Long Rest. You can Evaluate with each of your Professions once per Short Rest.

### Superior Tactics < lvl 2 >
Choose your preferred approach to solving dangerous problems from those listed below. At 5th and 8th level, you will gain additional abilities based on your choice.

#### Reposition
You quickly assess your surroundings for the best positioning. Stepping and Taking Cover cost 1 $[AP] for you.

#### Plot
You see your opponents' moves before they do. On your turn, you can spend all of your remaining $[AP]. You can then spend 1 $[AP] to take a Reaction at any point before you next turn. When you do, you gain temporary $[AP] equal to the $[AP] spent and can take any number of Actions in a row. Any unspent temporary $[AP] disappears when you finish taking Actions.

#### Retort
You respond to new situations at a moments notice. At the end of your turn, you can state a Trigger as if you were Preparing an action. If the Trigger occurs, you can take any Action with a base $[AP] cost of 2 or less as if it were a Reaction in response to the Trigger. 

<!--### Tactical Strike < lvl 3 >
When you make an Adroit Strike, you can reduce your Expert Bonus by any amount (but not below 0) before adding it to the DV of your weapon (it doesn’t remain reduced). If you do, you can use a Tactical maneuver that requires a Strike and costs 3 $[AP] or less as part of that Strike, without spending additional AP or any *Stamina*. The Maneuver must be of a level equal to or lower than the amount you reduced your Expert Bonus by.

### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >

You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 6th and 7th level.

### Elusive < lvl 4 >

Ever vigilant, you are difficult to pin. You gain the **Elude** Reaction, described below.

#### Elude

{{left
**Categories**   :: Tactical
**Cost**         :: Reaction, 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You can sense a creature hitting you, and you have more than 1 *Movement*
**Trigger**      :: You are hit by an Attack that targets your AC.
}}

You halve the damage taken from the triggering Attack after any Resistances have been applied.

### Consistency < lvl 5 >

When you make a Check using the Mod Die of a Hobby or Profession, if the value rolled on your Mod Die is below your Expert Bonus, you can replace it with your Expert Bonus.

### Flawless Execution < lvl 6 >

When you Evaluate any Check made as part of your Tactical Strike, the Check is Empowered.

### Prescient Perception < lvl 7 >

You have Blindsight out to 2 spaces, and your Elude action no longer has a CAP.

### Perfect Evaluation < lvl 8 >

When you Evaluate a Save or Skill Check, it is Empowered.

### Untouchable < lvl 9 >

While you have at least 1 $[AP] remaining, creatures you can sense within your $[PER] radius cannot have Advantage on Attack Checks against you. The Blindsight from your Prescient Perception also extends out to your $[PER] Radius.

### Masterclass < lvl 10 >

Once per Rest, when you make a Check using the Mod Die of a Hobby or Profession, you can use the maximum value of that Mod Die instead of rolling it.

### Paragon Facet < lvl 10 >

You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.-->

\column

## Expert Practices
In everything we do, there's always someone better. The Expert is that someone. An Expert's Practice is their arena, in which their skills are unmatched.

### Investigator
With eyes like a knife and a brain bright as the Sun at Apex, Investigators don't let nothin' slip. Best mind your P's and Q's, if you know what's good for ya.

#### Professional Investigator < lvl 1 >
You gain the Investigator Profession at Proficient Rank, or a Profession of your choice if you already have it.

#### Investigator’s Tools < lvl 1 >
Choose one type of Ranged weapon that lacks the Heavy and Massive properties. You can wield weapons of the chosen type, and they become part of your Expert’s Tools. The short range of thrown and ranged weapons in your Expert’s Tools is equal to your $[PER] Radius if it would be lower.

#### Eagle-Eyed < lvl 1 >
Searching and Detecting cost 1 $[AP] for you, and your $[PER] Attribute Checks are rolled in Tandem with your Tactical Die.

When you Strike, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] to Search for something on the target as part of the same Action, rolling your Attack Check before the PM rolls your $[PER] Check. If your Strike is a Success, you have Advantage on the Secret $[PER] Check.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

{{wide,text-align:center

## Gladiator

Masters of weaponry in all forms and trained to fight until they drop, gladiators are masters of the battlefield.

}}

{{classTable,wide

{{descriptive,text-align:left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Flame Alignment

#### Endurance Points
You start with 24 *Endurance* and gain 14 per level.

#### Training

**Actions**      :: You can take Combat Actions

**Armor**        :: You can add your Gladiator Bonus to your AC while wearing any kind of armor.

**Maneuvers**    :: Your Martial Die is your Maneuver Mod Die.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[PER] Save Checks and Save Checks for another Attribute of your choice in Tandem with your Martial Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PER] and Martial Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Weapons**      :: You can wield any weapon, and you can use your Martial Die in place of your Attribute Die for any Weapon Attack Check you make (it still counts as a Check of that Attribute).
}}


### Martial Training < lvl 1 >
Select your Martial Training from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Martial Training is the lessons of the people and places that turned you into a Gladiator. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Martial Maneuvers < lvl 1 >
Your path as a Gladiator allows you to execute maneuvers by spending ***Stamina***. You start with a maximum of 3 *Stamina*, and your maximum increases each level, as shown in the Stamina column of the Gladiator Class Table. When you execute a maneuver, you must spend *Stamina* equal to the maneuver’s level. You regain expended *Stamina* when you finish a Rest.

Choose three 1st-level maneuvers of the types you can learn as determined by your Martial Training for your initial maneuvers. You learn additional maneuvers each level as shown in the Maneuvers Known column of the Gladiator Class Table. The maximum maneuver level you're allowed to learn is shown in the Max Maneuver Level column on that table as well.

\column

### Fighter's Light < lvl 1 >
As a trained fighter, you have exceptional combat prowess and intuition. You gain the **Reignite** and **Size Up** Actions, described below.
:
##### Reignite
**Categories** :: Combat, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You haven't Reignited this turn

You reach deep to overcome fatigue. Roll your Martial Die in Tandem with your $[VIG] Die and add your level. You regain *Endurance* equal to the total.

You can Reignite a number of times equal to your Gladiator Bonus each Long Rest. You regain one spent use when you finish a Short Rest.
:
##### Size Up
**Categories** :: Combat, Concentrate, Secret
**Cost** :: 2 $[AP], Basic CAP

Choose a creature you can sense within your $[PER] Radius that you haven't Sized Up since your last Long Rest, then choose two of the statistics listed in the table below. The PM makes a Secret Basic $[PER] Check in Tandem with your Martial Die against the creature's $[PRS] for you.

::

{{column-count:2
|                   |
|:-----------------:|
| Armor Class       |
| Current Endurance |
| Level             |

|               |
|:-------------:|
| $[VIG] Points |
| $[AGI] Points |
| $[PER] Points |
}}

::

{{left
**Success:**            :: You learn if the creature is superior, inferior, or equal to you in the chosen statistics.
**Complete Success:**   :: You learn the exact value of the creature's chosen statistics.
**Failure:**            :: You are unable to ascertain anything.
**Complete Failure:**   :: You get a false sense of the creature's statistics.

}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Action Flare < lvl 2 >
On your turn, you can choose to gain temporary $[AP] equal to your Gladiator Bonus. When you do, you regain expended *Stamina* equal to half your Gladiator Bonus, and the CAP for all of your Actions resets. Any unspent temporary $[AP] disappears at the end of your turn.

Once you use this facet, you must finish a Rest before you can do so again. You gain an additional use of this facet between Rests at 8th level.

### Combat Style < lvl 2 >
Choose your preferred style of fighting, known as your **Combat Style** from those listed after the Class description. You become **Adept** in that Combat Style. When you reach 5th and 8th level, you can choose to gain a new one, or progress with a previously chosen Style, becoming **Expert**, then **Master**.

### Combat Styles
Gladiators are adept with all forms of weaponry, but they still have their favorites. The Combat Styles available to a Gladiator are described below.

#### Archery
The thrum of a loosed drawstring never sounded so deadly as yours.
:
**Adept** You can roll your $[PER] Die in Tandem with your Martial Die when you make an Attack Check with a Drawn weapon.

#### Defensive Fighting
The best offense is a good defense.
:
**Adept** The first time you make an Attack Check on each of your turns, you can choose to have that Attack Check and all others you make on that turn be Hindered 1. If you do, you gain a bonus to your AC equal to half your Gladiator Bonus until the beginning of your next turn.

#### Dueling
A proper duelist uses many forms.
:
**Adept** When you make an Attack Check with a Versatile weapon while wielding it with two hands, the Deadly DV increases by 1. When you Strike with a Versatile weapon while wielding it with one hand, you can Seize or Shove as part of that Action by spending an additional 1 $[AP], before or after your Attack Check.

\column

#### Fencer
You prefer your blades to be lithe and swift.
:
**Adept** You can roll your $[AGI] Die in Tandem with your Martial Die when you make an Attack Check with a Deft weapon.

#### Greatweapon Fighting
You specialize in wielding great hunks of steel that most would hesitate to call weapons.
:
**Adept** Attack Checks you make with Massive weapons are Bolstered 1.

#### Heavy Weapon Fighting
You favor true weapons of war that take both hands to master.
:
**Adept** You can roll your $[VIG] Die in Tandem with your Martial Die when you make an Attack Check with a Heavy melee weapon.

#### Juggernaut
You have the strength to wield larger weapons with ease.
:
**Adept** You can ignore the Two-Handed property for Heavy weapons you wield.

<!--**Expert** Your *Movement* is only reduced by 1 while wielding a Massive weapon, instead of 2. You also add half your $[VIG] points to the DV and Deadly DV of Massive weapons you wield.
:
**Master** When you roll at least 10 above the DC for an Attack Check with a Massive weapon, the target takes additional damage of the weapon’s type equal to the value of the d20.

### Efficient Strikes < lvl 3 >
Now a savant of weaponry, the first Action you take on each of your turns that involves Striking costs 1 less $[AP].

\column

### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >

You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 6th and 7th level.

### Unyielding < lvl 4 >

It takes a true opponent to fell you. When you suffer a Failure on a Save Check, you can Choose to make your result a Success. Once you use this feature, you must finish a Long Rest before you can use it again.

### Tireless < lvl 5 >

Unwavering in the face of mortal danger, you gain a new Action you can take, **Recover Stamina**, described below.

#### Recover Stamina

**Categories** :: Martial, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 2 $[AP], Basic CAP

You regain 1 spent *Stamina*. The Exhausted condition increases the $[AP] required to take this Action by its level.

### Adaptive Strikes < lvl 6 >

You learn from every mistake. When you take the Strike action on your turn and your Attack misses, you have Advantage on the next Attack Check you make against the same target that turn. The missed Strike also does not count against any CAP you have for Striking.

### Indomitable  < lvl 7 >

Defeat must earn you. When you use Unyielding, your result becomes a Complete Success instead of just a Success. You also gain an additional use of Unyielding between each Long Rest, and you regain one spent use of Unyielding each time you finish a Short Rest.

### Masterful Strikes < lvl 9 >

Few have ever attained your level of weapon mastery. When you make an Attack Check, you can treat any roll on the d20 lower than your Gladiator Bonus as your Gladiator Bonus. Natural 1s still Weaken the Check.

### Relentless < lvl 10 >

Nothing can stop you while you still draw breath. When you start your turn, you regain 1 spent *Stamina*.

### Paragon Facet  < lvl 10 >

You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.-->


![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}

## Martial Trainings

Each Gladiator is forged from the methods and environments that taught them the skills to succeed in combat. Their Training keeps them tuned to the rhythm of battle where others would be overwhelmed. 

### Victor
To the victor go the spoils, and there is no greater spoil than victory itself. You have and continue to train tirelessly, ever pushing yourself to be the greatest.

#### Surge to Victory < lvl 1 >
You can learn **Feral**, **Martial**, and **Instinctual** maneuvers.

Once per Rest, you can execute one of your maneuvers at your Max Maneuver Level without expending *Stamina*.

#### Practiced Combatant < lvl 1 >
Your Attack Checks score a Critical Success when you roll a 19 or 20 on the d20. You can also Step immediately after you score a Critical Success on an Attack Check without spending any $[AP].

#### Peerless Athlete  < lvl 1 >
Your dedication to maintaining your physique grants you the benefits below.
:
- Your Initiative Checks and $[VIG] Attribute Checks are rolled in Tandem with your Martial Die.

- Your Basic $[VIG] Skill Checks are Bolstered 1.

- Your $[VIG] Save Checks are Empowered.

<!--#### Extra Combat Style < lvl 4 >

You become Adept in an additional Combat Style of your choice.

#### Flawless Combatant < lvl 7 >

During an Encounter, you regain 1 spent *Radiance* when you start your turn with none left. Your Attack Checks also score a Critical Hit when you roll an 18 to 20 on the d20.

When you score a Critical Hit, you can maximize the value of your Mod Dice, but you must finish a Long Rest before you can do so again.

#### Undefeatable < lvl 10 >

The world itself can't help but root for you. When you start your turn Bloodied, Soma Light fills you, causing you to regain *Endurance* equal to 5 plus your $[VIG] Points.-->

#### Defensive Fighting

The first time you make an Attack Check on each of your turns, you can choose to have that Attack Check and all others you make on that turn be Hindered 1. If you do, you gain a bonus to your AC equal to half your Gladiator Bonus until the beginning of your next turn.

#### Dueling

When you make an Attack Check with a Versatile weapon while wielding it with two hands, the Deadly DV increases by 1. {{wide,text-align:center

## Incarnate

Mortals that share a soul with one of the ancient Primordials. These fate-chosen individuals are sources of omen and legend alike.

}}


{{classTable,wide
##### Incarnate Class Table
|       | Incarnate |                                               | Primordial |         | Manuevers | Max Manevuer |
| Level | Bonus     | Facets                                        | Die     | Stamina | Known     | Level        |
|:-----:|:--:|:-----------------------------------------------------|:-------:|:-------:|:---------:|:------------:|
|  1st  | +2 | Primordial Soul, Incarnate Maneuvers, Primeval Light |  1d6    |    3    |     2     |      1st     |
|  2nd  | +2 | Manifestations, Titanus Form                         |  1d6    |    4    |     3     |      1st     |
<!--|  3rd  | +3 | Resonance                                    |  1d6    |    3    |     3     |      2nd     |-->
}}

{{descriptive,text-align:left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Temporal Alignment.

#### Endurance Points
You start with 20 *Endurance* and gain 10 per level.

#### Training

**Armor**        :: You can add your Incarnate level to your AC while not wearing armor. You can also add your Incarnate Bonus to your AC while wearing Light armor.

**Maneuvers**    :: Your Primordial Die is your Maneuver Mod Die.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[PER] and $[PRS] Save Checks in Tandem with your Primordial Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PER] and Primordial Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Your Primordial Die is your Spell Mod Die. Checks you make using your Spell Mod Die count as $[PRS] Checks.
}}

### Primordial Soul < lvl 1 >
Select your Primordial Soul from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Primordial Soul is the ancient creature with which you share your soul. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 2nd, 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

Your Primordial Soul will allow you to cast certain spells, using the **Channel** Action, described below.
:
##### Channel
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate
**Cost** :: $[AP] in spells Cast Time
**Requirements** :: You haven't Channeled this turn

You cast one of your cantrips or Soul Spells. Channeled spells have no Components, and you can Exalt them by spending 1 *Stamina* per Exalted level, up to your Max Maneuver Level.

Once you Channel a leveled spell, you can’t Channel that spell again until you finish a Long Rest.

\column

### Incarnate Maneuvers < lvl 1 >
Your enhanced senses allow you to execute **Feral** and **Instinctual** maneuvers by spending ***Stamina***. You start with a maximum of 3 *Stamina*, and your maximum increases each level, as shown in the Stamina column of the Incarnate Class Table. When you execute a maneuver, you must spend *Stamina* equal to the maneuver’s level. You regain expended *Stamina* when you finish a Rest.

Choose two 1st-level maneuvers of the types you can learn (Feral and Instinctual) as your initial maneuvers. You learn an additional maneuver each level as shown in the Maneuvers Known column of the Incarnate Class Table. The maximum maneuver level you're allowed to learn is shown in the Max Maneuver Level column on that table as well.

### Primeval Light < lvl 1 >
The primeval savagery of your primordial grants you the facets below.

#### Ferocious Strikes
You can replace your $[VIG] Die with your Primordial Die when you Unarmed Strike (it's still a $[VIG] Check). If you do, the DV equals half your Incarnate Bonus, and the Deadly DV is your Incarnate Bonus.

#### Primeval Force
The first time on each of your turns that you make a Successful Unarmed Strike, you deal additional Sonic damage to the target and gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to your $[PRS] Points.

#### Primal Reflexes
When you roll Initiative, you can Stride as a Reaction. If you do, your Initiative Check is rolled in Tandem with your Primordial Die.

### Manifestations <lvl 2 >
Features of your Primordial have begun to appear on your body, granting you supernatural ability. Choose an Attribute for your **Manifestations** to improve. You gain the first tier of benefits for that Attribute as listed after the Class description.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

Every even level after this one, you choose an Attribute again, gaining the next tier of benefits if you’ve selected it before.

Each Manifestation you gain causes a noticeable physical change of your choice on your body, reflective of your Primordial Soul. 

### Titanus Form < lvl 2 >
You gain the Titan Morph Action, described below

##### Titan Morph
{{left
**Categories** :: Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: You have enough unoccupied adjacent spaces to increase your Size; You haven't Titan Morphed since your last Long Rest.
}}

Spend any amount of $[AP] up to your Max Maneuver Level. You enter your **Titanus Form**, the benefits of which are described in your Primordial Soul. During your Titanus Form, your Size increases based on the amount of $[AP] you spent, as shown in the table below.

::

|AP|Size|
|:-:|:-:| 
|1|Gigantic|
|2|Enormous|
|3|Gargantuan|
|4|Colossal|
|5|Titanic|

::

The amount of $[AP] you spent becomes your **Size Bonus** for the duration of your Titanus Form, which affects you in the ways below.

:

- ***Checks.*** Your $[VIG] and $[PER] Checks are Bolstered with a level equal to your Size Bonus.

- ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* increases by your Size Bonus.

- ***Reach.*** Your Melee Attack Checks can reach targets an additional number of spaces away from you equal to your Size Bonus.

- ***Resistance.*** You have Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing Resistance equal to twice your Size Bonus.

- ***Stamina.*** You regain spent *Stamina* equal to your Size Bonus at the end of your turn.

- ***Temporary Endurance.*** When you Titan Morph, you gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to 10 times your Size Bonus.

:

During your Titanus Form, any equipment you are wearing becomes unusable, and you are unarmored. You remain in your Titanus Form for 1 minute, and it only ends early if you die.

\column

<!--### Resonance < lvl 3 >
The first time you Strike during your turn, you can Stride or Jump as part of the same Action. If you move at least 3 spaces before making your Weapon Attack Check, it gains additional effects, described below.

{{left
**Success** :: You may have all damage dealt by your weapon become Sonic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, and the target is Stunned 1, or Stunned 2 if the target is smaller than you.
}}-->

### Manifestations
#### Vigor
##### 1st Manifestation
Your Endurance Maximum gains a bonus equal to twice your Level.
:
You also gain one Bio cantrip of your choice. You may Channel it for 2 $[AP] by spending 1 *Stamina* as you do.
<!--##### 2nd Manifestation
You gain 1 $[VIG] Point.-->

#### Agility
##### 1st Manifestation
Stepping costs 1 $[AP] for you.
:
You also learn one Martial Maneuver.
<!--##### 2nd Manifestation
You gain 1 $[AGI] Point.-->

#### Perception
##### 1st Manifestation
You have Night Vision.
:
You also gain one Mind cantrip of your choice. You may Channel it for 2 $[AP] by spending 1 *Stamina* as you do.

<!--##### 2nd Manifestation
You gain 1 $[PER] Point.-->

#### Intelligence
##### 1st Manifestation
You roll $[INT] Save Checks in Tandem with your Primordial Die.
:
You also learn one Tactical Maneuver.
<!--##### 2nd Manifestation
You gain 1 $[INT] Point.-->

#### Presence
##### 1st Manifestation
You roll $[PRS] Attribute Checks in Tandem with your Primordial Die.
:
You also learn one Psychological Maneuver.

<!--##### 2nd Manifestation
You gain 1 $[PRS] Point.-->

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

## Primordial Souls
Pagan apocrypha and raving zealots tell the stories of ancient titans. Creatures that roamed the Prism before even the gods, supposedly. Incarnates give more weight to those stories than Prismatic adherents are comfortable with.


### The King
Ancient lord of ice and beast from before the dominion of gods, the King ruled as the strongest Primordial, keeping order through force. Through your connected souls, you feel the chilling might of an endless iceberg. 

#### Soul Spells < lvl 1 >
The King gives you the icy soul of a predator. You learn two Frost evocations, which you can cast by Channeling. You learn an additional Frost Evocation each time your Incarnate bonus increases.

#### Amphibious < lvl 1 >
You gain *Swim Movement* equal to your *Movement*, and can breathe underwater. If you already have *Swim Movement*, it increases by 2 spaces.

#### King of Beasts < lvl 1 >
You share the soul of the beast king, granting you the benefits below.

- ***Alignment.*** You gain a level of Frost Alignment.

- ***Actions.*** You can take Combat Actions.

- ***Beast Lord.*** Any $[PRS] Skill Checks you make to interact with Beasts are Empowered.

- ***Claws.*** When you Unarmed Strike, you can deal Slashing damage instead of Crushing damage.

- ***Cold.*** You can deal Arctic damage or Sonic damage with Primeval Force.

\column

#### Titanus Form < lvl 2 >
When you Titan Morph, your body takes on the form of the Primordial King, a great amalgous beast. While in your Titanus Form, your body gains the Pushing property for Unarmed Strikes, and you can Channel the **Primordial Breath** spell, described below.

:

##### Primordial Breath
*Ice Cantrip*

**Cast Time** :: Varies
**Range** :: 10 times your Size Bonus
**Components** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
:
Spend 3 to 5 $[AP], then spend *Stamina* up to your Incarnate Bonus (minimum 1). You exhale a concentrated beam of Frost Light. Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within range. Add your Mod Die to the Arctic damage dealt.

:

{{left
**Success** :: Arctic damage equal to the $[AP] spent times the *Stamina* spent, Freezing with a level equal to the *Stamina* spent.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, additional Sonic damage equal to half the Arctic damage dealt, Freezing level increases by 1.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center
## Luminary
Natural fonts of radiance, Luminaries channel their inner light with an ease few arcane practitioners can match.
}}

{{classTable,wide
##### Luminary Class Table
|       | Luminary    |                                              | Lumen   | Light  | Max Spell |
| Level | Bonus       | Facets                                       | Die     | Limit  | Level     |
|:-----:|:--:|:------------------------------------------------------|:-------:|:------:|:---------:|
|  1st  | +2 | Inner Luminance, Natural Radiance, Focusing           |   1d6   |   3    |    1st    |
|  2nd  | +2 | Convex Focus, Shining Soul                            |   1d6   |   4    |    1st    |
<!--|  3rd  | +3 | —                                                     |   1d6   |   7    |    2nd    |
|  4th  | +3 | Luminance Facet, Brilliance, Adventurer Facet         |   1d8   |   8    |    2nd    |
|  5th  | +4 | Concave Focus                                         |   1d8   |   10   |    3rd    |
|  6th  | +4 | Incandescence                                         |   1d8   |   11   |    3rd    |
|  7th  | +5 | Luminance Facet, Beaming Brilliance, Adventurer Facet |   1d10  |   14   |    4th    |
|  8th  | +5 | Star Lens                                             |   1d10  |   15   |    4th    |
|  9th  | +6 | —                                                     |   1d10  |   17   |    5th    |
| 10th  | +6 | Luminance Facet, Blinding Brilliance, Paragon Facet   |   1d12  |   18   |    5th    |-->
}}

{{left
#### Endurance Points
You start with 18 *Endurance* and gain 8 per level.

#### Training
**Actions**       :: You can take Arcane Actions.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[VIG] and $[PRS] Save Checks in Tandem with your Lumen Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[VIG] and Lumen Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Choose $[VIG] or $[PRS] (this can’t be changed). That Attribute Die is your Spell Mod Die, and Checks you make using your Spell Mode Die count as Checks of that Attribute.
}}

### Inner Luminance < lvl 1 >
Select your Inner Luminance from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Luminance is the type of Light that shines within you. It grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Natural Radiance < lvl 1 >
Imbued with Light, you exude a natural radiance. You gain additional *Radiance* equal to half your Luminary Bonus.

When you finish a Rest, you regain spent *Radiance* equal to half your Luminary Bonus.

### Focusing < lvl 1 >
You can cast spells by using your body as a focusing lens for your inner ***Light***. 

#### Learning Spells
Your Inner Luminance determines the spells you learn each level. The maximum spell level you’re allowed to learn is shown in the Max Spell Level column on the Luminary Class Table.

#### Casting Spells
Luminaries focus the Light within themselves to cast spells. The total amount of *Light* you can spend each Long Rest is listed in the Light Limit column on the Luminary Class Table. Each time you finish a Short Rest, the amount of *Light* applied to your Light Limit is reduced by your Luminary Bonus.

\column

You gain *Light* with the **Focus** Action, and you cast spells with the **Emit** Action, each described below.

##### Focus
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate
**Cost** :: $[AP] equal to half the chosen level

Choose a level up to your Max Spell Level minus your current *Light*. You gain that much *Light*. Unspent *Light* dissipates at the end of your next turn.
:
##### Emit
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** ::$[SL] *Light*, $[AP] in the spell's Cast Time

You cast one of your cantrips or learned Spells at a level up to your Max Spell Level. Spells you Emit have no Charged Gem, Hand, or Spoken requirements. When you Emit a spell with a Powdered Gem requirement, you can spend 1 additional *Light* per 2g of Powdered Gem in place of that component.

#### Ray Blast
You can focus your inner Light as a weapon. You gain the **Ray Blast** Action, described below.
:
##### Ray Blast
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: You have not Ray Blasted this turn.

You unleash a potent ray of light. Spend any amount of $[AP] and make a Spell Attack Check against a creature you can sense within your $[PER] Radius plus your Luminary Bonus. The damage type and target Attribute are determined by your Inner Luminance. Add your Mod Die to the damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Damage equal to half your Luminary Bonus per $[AP] spent.
**Deadly Success** :: Damage equal to your Luminary Bonus per $[AP] spent.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Convex Focus < lvl 2 >
When you Emit or Ray Blast, you may spend an additional 1 $[AP] to roll your Lumen Die in Tandem with your Mod Die for any Check made as part of that Action.

### Shining Soul < lvl 2 >
Your soul shines bright, even in your darkest moments. When you spend a *Radiance*, you gain the benefits below.

- ***Heal*** You regain *Endurance* equal to your Luminary Level.

- ***Refresh*** The amount of *Light* applied to your Light Limit is reduced by 1.

- ***Shine*** You gain 1 *Light*, which dissipates at the end of your next turn.

<!--### Brilliance < lvl 4 >
You gain the **Unleash Brilliance** action, described below.
:
##### Unleash Brilliance
{{left
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 5 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: You have not Unleashed Brilliance since your last Long Rest.
}}

You fully unleash your Light, becoming **Brilliant** and regaining spent *Radiance* equal to your Luminary Bonus. While Brilliant, you gain the benefits below.

:

* ***Armor Class.*** Your AC equals your Luminary level plus your $[VIG] and $[PRS] if it isn’t already higher.

* ***Bright.*** You glow with the color of your Inner Luminance, creating Full Brightness out to your Luminary Bonus.

* ***Extra Damage.*** The first time you deal damage with a spell or Ray Blast each turn, the damage increases by your Luminary Bonus.

* ***Focus Checks.*** Any Checks you make to Focus or Ray Blast are rolled in Tandem with your $[VIG] or $[PRS] Die.

:

You remain Brilliant for 1 minute or until you end your turn with no *Light*.

### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >
You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 7th level.

### Concave Focus < lvl 5 >
When you make an Attack Check for a Focused spell or Ray Blast, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] and 1 *Light* any number of times to target an additional creature you can sense within range per *Light* spent.

When you Focus a spell that affects an area, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] and 1 *Light* to increase the radius or size of the area in one dimension by your Luminary Bonus.

\column

### Incandescence < lvl 6 >
Overflowing with Light, you shine beyond measure. Your Natural Radiance gives you additional *Radiance* equal to your Luminary Bonus, instead of half.

### Beaming Brilliance < lvl 7 >
Once on each of your turns while Brilliant, you can spend 1 *Light* to Empower a Check you make at Advantage.

You can also Unleash Brilliance as long as you have not done so since your last Short Rest, rather than your last Long Rest.

### Star Lens < lvl 8 >
Your light is a beacon, burning bright as the stars above. You can spend 1 *Radiance* in place of the $[AP] required to Sustain a Focused spell.

### Blinding Brilliance < lvl 10 >
While Brilliant, you regain 1 spent *Radiance* at the start of your turn.

### Paragon Facet < lvl 10 >
You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

## Inner Luminance
The colors of the Primos shine brighter in some than others. Luminaries shine brightest of all, each with their own Inner Luminance.

### Flaming Luminance
Filled with the burning radiance of Flame Light, these lumens conjure blaze as easily as breath.

#### Inner Flame < lvl 1 >
As a natural source of Flame Light, the spells you learn follow the rules below.
:
- ***Initial Arcana.*** You learn two Fire cantrips and two Fire Arcana.

- ***Level Up.*** You learn an additional Fire Arcana each time you gain a Lumen level. If that level is odd, you learn one more.

\column

#### Enduring Warmth < lvl 1 >
Your inner flame grants you the benefits below.
:
- ***Flame Alignment.*** You gain a level of Flame Alignment.

- ***Flame Manipulation.*** Sustaining a Fire cantrip costs 1 less $[AP] for you (minimum 0 $[AP]). You can also Interact with a creature and spend an additional 1 $[AP] to end the Burning condition on a creature you can touch.

- ***Ray Blast.*** Your Ray Blast targets $[PER] and deals Thermic damage. You can spend an additional 1 $[AP] and 1 *Light* when you Ray Blast to add Burning to the Success effects with a level equal to half your Luminary Bonus, or that level plus 1 on a Deadly Success.

- ***Thermic Resistance.*** You have Thermic Resistance equal to your Lumen Bonus.

#### Eruption < lvl 1 >
You gain the **Erupt** Action, described below.
:
##### Erupt
{{left
**Categories** :: Arcane, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: You have not Erupted since your last Rest
}}

You explode with Flame Light. Spend 3 to 5 $[AP]. Each creature in an emanation out to your Luminary Bonus makes a $[PER] Save Check against your Spell DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Thermic damage equal to your Luminary Bonus per $[AP] spent.
**Deadly Failure** :: As a Success, additional Thermic damage equal to half your Luminary Bonus per $[AP] spent.
**Success** :: Damage equal to half your Luminary Bonus per $[AP] spent.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
}}

<!--### Molten Luminance

If that level is odd, you learn one additional arcana of your choice.

Your spellcasting is also affected in the following ways.

:

* Your Ray Blast targets $[PRS] and deals Profane damage.

* When you finish a Long Rest, you learn a randomly determined cantrip. You forget it the next time you finish a Long Rest.

Nothing like you should exist. You gain the following abilities.

:

* When you Focus, you may replace any damage types other than Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing dealt by the spell with Profane damage.

* When you Focus while Brilliant, you may end your Brilliance to cast any spell you could learn, without spending any *Light*.-->
