{{wide,text-align:center

## Thaumaturge

Miracle workers both benevolent and destructive, Thaumaturges are the most prominent conduits of divine will.

}}

{{classTable,wide
##### Thaumaturge Class Table
|       | Thaumaturge |                                       | Miracle | Favor | Max   |
| Level | Bonus       | Facets                                | Die     | Limit | Favor |
|:-----:|:--:|:-----------------------------------------------|:-------:|:-----:|:-----:|
|  1st  | +2 | Divine Domain, Thaumaturgy, Clerical Celebrant |   1d6   |   2   |   1   |
|  2nd  | +2 | Favored Acolyte, Entreating Prayer             |   1d6   |   3   |   1   |
<!--|  3rd  | +3 | Divine Strikes                                 |   1d6   |   5   |   2   |
|  4th  | +3 | Domain Facet, Avatar, Adventurer Facet         |   1d8   |   6   |   2   |
|  5th  | +4 | Greater Miracle                                |   1d8   |   8   |   3   |
|  6th  | +4 | Unwavering Devotion                            |   1d8   |   9   |   3   |
|  7th  | +5 | Domain Facet, Greater Avatar, Adventurer Facet |   1d10  |   11  |   4   |
|  8th  | +5 | Archpriest                                     |   1d10  |   12  |   4   |
|  9th  | +6 | —                                              |   1d10  |   14  |   5   |
| 10th  | +6 | Domain Facet, Perfect Avatar, Paragon Facet    |   1d12  |   15  |   5   |-->
}}

{{left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Infernal Alignment

#### Endurance Points
You start with 20 *Endurance* and gain 10 per level.

#### Training
**Actions**      :: You can take Supernatural Actions.

**Armor**        :: You can add your Thaumaturge Bonus to your AC while wearing Light or Medium armor.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[PRS] Save Checks in Tandem with your Miracle Die, and they are Empowered.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PRS] and Miracle Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Your Miracle Die is your Spell Mod Die. Checks you make using your Spell Mod Die count as $[PRS] Checks.

**Weapons**      :: You can wield any melee weapon that lacks the Two-Handed property.

}}

### Divine Domain < lvl 1 >
Select your Domain from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Domain represents a piece of reality your deity controls, around which their worship is focused. Your Domain grants you facets when you select it, and again at 2nd, 3rd,  4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Thaumaturgy < lvl 1 >
As a Thaumaturge, you are able to cast spells by garnering ***Favor*** from your deity.

#### Preparing spells
Each Long Rest, you can **Prepare** a number of spells from your Domain Spells equal to your Thaumaturge Level plus your $[PRS] points, allowing you to cast those spells until your next Long Rest. Spells Prepared this way must have a level no higher than your Max Favor, as shown in the Thaumaturge Class Table.

\column

#### Casting Spells
Thaumaturges entreat spells from the *Favor* granted by their deity. The total amount of *Favor* you can spend each Long Rest is listed in the Favor Limit column on the Thaumaturge Class Table. Each time you finish a Short Rest, the amount of *Favor* applied to your Favor Limit is reduced by your Thaumaturge Bonus.

You garner *Favor* using the **Pray** Action, and you cast spells with the **Evoke** Action, each described below.
:
##### Pray
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 1 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: Two hands or a holy symbol of your deity
}}

You pray to your deity, garnering 1 *Favor* if you aren’t **Indebted** and your Favor Limit hasn't been reached. Regardless of any *Favor* garnered, you are also blessed with the benefits below (these stack with each Prayer).
:
- ***Bolstered Save.*** The first Save Check you make before your next turn is Bolstered 1.

- ***Hindered Attack.*** The first Attack Check made against you before your next turn is Hindered 1.

Unspent *Favor* dissipates at the start of your next turn.
:
##### Evoke
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: $[SL] *Favor*, $[AP] in spell’s Cast Time reduced by $[AP] spent Praying this turn
**Requirements** :: Two hands or a holy symbol of your deity; You haven’t Evoked or made a Guided Strike this turn
}}

You cast one of your mantras or Prepared spells at a level up to your Max Favor. The spell requires two hands unless you are holding a holy symbol of your deity.

You then become Indebted 2$[SL]. Each time you Sustain the spell, you regain that Indebted level.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Divine Debt
When you Evoke or Sustain an Evoked spell, you become Indebted 2$[SL]. The Condition is described below.

##### Indebted X
When you Pray, no *Favor* is garnered.

At the start of your turn, roll your Miracle Die. If the result is higher than X, this Condition ends. At the end of your turn, the level of this Condition reduces by 1 for every 2 $[AP] spent Praying that turn if you didn't Evoke.

### Clerical Celebrant < lvl 1 >
As a divinely ordained minister of your deity, you can perform rites to identify the presence of supernatural forces. When you Identify Supernatural, the PM rolls your Miracle Die in Tandem with your $[PRS] Die for the Secret Check, which is Empowered. You also gain the **Detect Supernatural** Action, described below.
:
##### Detect Supernatural
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 1 *Favor*, 2 $[AP]

You become aware of supernatural afflictions within your $[PER] Radius for 10 minutes. During that time, objects, areas, and creatures with supernatural afflictions or that hide objects with such afflictions glow faintly to you. You can spend 2 $[AP] to take the Identify Supernatural action against them as long as they are within your $[PER] Radius. You cannot Detect Supernatural through 1 inch of metal, 1 foot of stone, or 3 feet of wood or dirt.

### Favored Acolyte < lvl 2 >
A devout and proven follower, your deity grants you special attention. You gain the **Entreat Miracle** Action, described below.
:
##### Entreat Miracle

{{left
**Categories**   :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost**         :: 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: Two hands or a holy symbol of your deity; You haven't Entreated a Miracle since your last Rest
}}

You pray for a miracle. Your Indebted level is reduced by your Thaumaturge Bonus, and you choose **Protect Allies** or **Rebuke Oblux**, described below. Your Domain will grant you an additional option.

\column

###### Protect Allies
Roll your Miracle Die and your $[PRS] Die, add the rolled values together, and choose a number of creatures up to your Thaumaturge Bonus that you can sense within your $[PER] Radius. The chosen creatures gain the benefits below.
:
- ***Endurance.*** They regain *Endurance* equal to the total rolled plus twice your Thaumaturge level.

- ***AC Bonus.*** Their ACs increase by your Thaumaturge Bonus until the start of your next turn.

- ***Tandem Saves.*** Save Checks they make before your next turn are rolled in Tandem with your Miracle Die.
:
###### Rebuke Oblux
Each Obluminous creature in an emanation out to your $[PRS] points makes a $[PRS] Save against your Spell DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure**            :: Divine damage equal to twice your Thaumaturge Level, Blinded until the start of your next turn, Stunned 4.
**Deadly Failure**     :: Divine damage equal to three times your Thaumaturge Level, Blinded until the start of your next turn, Stunned 5.
**Success**            :: Divine damage equal to your Thaumaturge Level, Stunned 2.
**Complete Success**   :: Half as much damage as a Success, Stunned 1.
}}

### Entreating Prayer < lvl 2 >
When you Pray during an Encounter, you can choose to spend an additional 1 $[AP] to shout your prayer as loud as you can, giving that Prayer the Spoken requirement. If you do, you can roll your $[PRS] Die in Tandem with your Miracle Die for any Check made as part of Evoking a spell that turn. Your Indebted level then increases by 2 after Evoking it.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

<!--### Guided Strikes < lvl 3 >
Your fist is guided by divine force. You gain the **Guided Strike** Action, described below.
:
##### Guided Strike
{{left
**Categories**   :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Move, Hostile
**Cost**         :: 2 $[AP] reduced by the $[AP] spent Praying this turn
**Requirements** :: You haven’t Evoked or made a Guided Strike this turn.
}}

You Strike, using your Miracle Die as the Mod Die for the Attack Check. On a Success, the damage type of your weapon is replaced with a damage type determined by your Domain.

If you spent at least 3 $[AP] Praying this turn, the Attack Check is rolled in Tandem with your PRS Die.

### Avatar < lvl 4 >
So great is your faith that in times of need, you can become a minor avatar of your deity. You gain the **Manifest Presence** Action, described below.
:
##### Manifest Presence
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: 5 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: Two hands or a holy symbol of your deity; You haven’t Prayed this turn; You haven’t Manifested Presence since your last Long Rest
}}

You enter your **Avatar Form**, which lasts for 10 minutes or until you reach your Light Limit. When you enter your Avatar Form, you lose the Indebted condition, and the amount of *Light* applied to your Light Limit is reduced by your Thaumaturge Bonus.

During your Avatar Form, you can’t Pray, and you gain the benefits below.
:
* ***Armor Class.*** Your AC increases by half your Thaumaturge Bonus.

* ***Guided Strikes.*** You can Guided Strike any number of times each turn, and the Attack Check is rolled in Tandem with your PRS Die.

* ***Indebted Immunity.*** You are Immune to the Indebted Condition.

* ***Save Checks.*** Your Save Checks are rolled in Tandem with your Miracle Die.

* ***Miracles.*** You can Entreat Miracles any number of times each turn by spending 2 *Light*.

### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >
You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 6th and 7th level.

\column

### Greater Miracle < lvl 5 >
You can call upon even greater favors from your god. When you Entreat a Miracle, you can choose the **Divine Intervention** option, described below.
:
###### Divine Intervention
Choose any spell you could Prepare with Thaumaturgy. You cast that spell, without spending any $[AP] or meeting any of the spell’s requirements, and the spell is cast at a level equal to your Max Light. You can use this option for Evoke Miracle once per Long Rest.

### Unwavering Devotion < lvl 6 >
Your faith is unmatched, and the light of your deity shines upon you. When you roll your Miracle Die at the start of your turn to see if you are no longer Indebted, it is rolled in Tandem with your $[PRS] Die.

### Greater Avatar < lvl 7 >
The power of your Avatar Form increases as your deity’s faith in you grows. Your Avatar Form gains additional benefits, listed below.
:
* ***Tandem Spell Checks.*** When you Evoke, any Checks you make for the spell are rolled in Tandem with your $[PRS] Die.

* ***Empowered Evocation.*** When you Evoke, you can spend 1 additional *Light* to Empower a single Check made for that spell.

* ***Empowered Strikes.*** When you Guided Strike, you can spend 1 additional $[AP] to Empower the Attack Check.

### Archpriest < lvl 8 >
Your deity has anointed you a world leader of their faith. You can Entreat a Miracle twice per Rest.

When you Entreat a Divine Intervention Miracle, you can choose any spell of a type you could prepare, regardless of level. The spell is cast at a level equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus, and Checks you make as part of casting it are rolled in Tandem with your $[PRS] Die. They are also Empowered.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--### Perfect Avatar < lvl 10 >
You have become a symbol of divine authority, destined to be at the right hand of your lord. You can Manifest Presence twice per Long Rest, and your Avatar Form gains additional benefits, listed below.
:
* ***Endurance.*** At the start of your turn, you regain *Endurance* equal to your Thaumaturge Level.

* ***Healing Evocation.*** Once on each of your turns, you can spend 1 $[AP] to restore *Endurance* equal to your Thaumaturge Level to an ally you can sense within your $[PER] Radius.

* ***Temporary AP.*** At the end of your turn, you gain 1 temporary $[AP]. It disappears at the end of your next turn if unused.

### Paragon Facet  < lvl 10 >
You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.-->

## Divine Domains
Thaumaturges are made from the most devout followers of the deities that make up the Prismatic Pantheon. The abilities of a Thaumaturge depend on the Divine Domain of their god, which describe their sphere of influence over the Prismos.

### Cosmos Domain
For eons, denizens of the facets have whispered of the secrets held within the Moon. They say praying to the its master, Lunaeus, god of knowledge and trickery, can bring great wisdom. Sometimes, too great.

#### Domain Spells < lvl 1 >
As a Thaumaturge of the cosmos, you can Prepare Frost, Pneuma, Lunar, and Platinum evocations with your Thaumaturgy feature, as well as Mind and Moon arcana.

You also learn three mantras, each of which must be either Frost, Pneuma, Lunar, or Platinum. You can choose to learn a Mind or Moon cantrip in place of any number of these mantras.

#### Guiding Moonlight < lvl 1 >
When you make an $[INT] Attribute Check, it is Bolstered with a level equal to half your Thaumaturge Bonus.

#### Cosmic Knowledge < lvl 1 >
Once per turn after you deal Cosmic damage, you can roll your $[INT] Die in Tandem with your Miracle Die. You gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to the result.

Also, when you would deal Psychic damage, you can choose to deal Cosmic damage instead. 

#### Lunar Miracle < lvl 2 >
When you Entreat a Miracle, you can choose the **Reveal Secrets** option, described below.
:
###### Reveal Secrets
For 1 minute, Moonlight fills the area around you in an emanation with a size equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus plus 1. Any $[INT] Skill Checks made by you or an Ally within the emanation are Bolstered 1, and any Hostile Invisible creatures and objects within it glow with blue light.

Anything within the emanation that was made or altered to have a secret meaning, including text, iconography, pictures, or objects, glows blue. For example, the text of an innocuous letter that is actually coded language would glow, however nothing about the true meaning of the text is revealed.

<!--#### Cosmic Strikes < lvl 3 >
Your Guided Strike deals cosmic damage instead of your weapon's normal damage type, and when you make a Guided Strike, you can spend 1 *Light*. If you do, your Attack Check is against the target's $[INT] instead of its AC, and it gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success**          :: Morphing 1.
**Deadly Success**   :: Morphing 2.
**Failure**          :: 3 Cosmic damage (add the value of your Mod Die as with a Successful Strike).
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing
}}

#### Vexing Revelations < lvl 4 >
When you Pray, you can sacrifice *Temporary Endurance* to use one of the effects described below. The amount you must sacrifice is listed beside the name of the effect.
:
{{left
**Fold Space (4)** :: You Teleport to a space you can sense within a number of spaces equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus.

**Fade Away (8)** :: You become Invisible until the end of your next turn or until you spend $[AP] for any Action other than Praying or a Move action.

**Share Knowledge (Any)** :: You grant a creature you can sense within your $[PER] Radius *Temporary Endurance* equal to the amount you sacrificed.
}}

Additionally, during your Avatar Form the amount of *Temporary Endurance* you gain from Cosmic Knowledge is doubled, and you can use the any of the above effects by spending 1 $[AP] instead of Praying (this does not end your Invisibility from Fade Away).

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

Your Vexing Revelations gain additional effects, described below.
:

**Fold Space** You can touch another creature to Teleport them with you, provided it is your size or smaller. The creature appears in a space adjacent to the one you Teleport to.
:
**Fade Away** You can have a creature you can sense within your $[PER] Radius become Invisible instead of you. The creature remains Invisible until it spends $[AP] for any Action other than a Move action.
:
**Share Knowledge** You can target a second creature you can sense wtihin your $[PER] Radius to gains the same amount of *Temporary Endurance*

:
{{left
**Failure**          :: Morphing 1.
**Deadly Failure**   :: Morphing 2.
**Success**          :: Nothing.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing, and the creature is Immune to this effect for 1 day.
}}

#### Bishop of the Cosmos < lvl 10 >
When you inflict the Morphing condition on a creature, the level of that condition increases by 1, or 2 during your Avatar Form.

When you roll to gain *Temporary Endurance* with your Cosmic Knowledge facet, you can replace the value rolled with your Thaumaturge Bonus if it is lower.

### Forge Domain
Generations of connection, forged through hammer and flame. The forge of civilization is ever burning, and from its crucible a better tomorrow is cast.

#### Domain Spells < lvl 1 >
As a Thaumaturge of the Forge, you can Prepare Flame, Pneuma, Velvet, Temporal, and Platinum Evocations with your Thaumaturgy feature.

You also learn three mantras, each of which must be either Flame, Pneuma, Velvet, Temporal, or Platinum.

#### Hammerheart < lvl 1 >
Once per turn when you deal Thermic or Crushing damage with an Attack Check, you gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to the value of your Mod Die for the Check.

\column

You can also add your Thaumaturge Bonus to your AC while wearing and wield any melee weapon with the Bludgeoning property.

#### Pillars of Creation < lvl 1 >
You gain the **Create Structure** Action, described below.
:
##### Create Structure
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate
**Cost** :: 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: Spoken, Two hands or a holy symbol of your deity
}}

Choose a space on the ground you can sense within your $[PER] Radius. A **Miraculous Structure** rises from that space, occupying it. Then, if there are more Miraculous Structures than your Thaumaturge Level, the oldest one crumbles to dust.

You can choose the shape of the structure, if the structure fully blockades its space or is passable, and whether a creature can stand atop it. If you create a structure beneath a creature, they can choose to Step into an unoccupied space as a Reaction, or be raised with the structure.

Your Miraculous Structures are 1 space high and use the stat block below. You can also stack your Miraculous Structures to form a tower. You can have a number of Structures stacked on top of each other equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus.
:
###### Miraculous Structure
*Medium Object*, *Stone (Hard)*
::
| AGI  | PER  | INT  | PRS  |
|:----:|:----:|:----:|:----:|
|-5 (5)|-5 (5)|-5 (5)|-5 (5)|
::
{{left
**Armor Class** :: 3 (immobile)
**Endurance** :: 10 plus your Thaumaturge Level

**Resistances** :: Arctic 50, Caustic 10, Crushing 5, Galvanic 10, Piercing 5, Slashing 10, Thermic 50
**Vulnerabilities** :: Profane 3d6
**Immunities** :: Cosmic, Chthonic, Psychic, Somatic
}}
:
###### Traits
**Object** The object has no $[AP] and cannot gain $[AP].

#### Molten Miracle < lvl 2 >
When you Entreat a Miracle, you can choose the **Raise Pillars** option, described below.
:
###### Raise Pillars
You Create Structures a number of times equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus without spending any $[AP]. Any Miraculous Structures of your choice then begin burning with immense heat.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--For 1 minute, your Miraculous Structures gain an emanation out to your $[PRS] Points. A creature that starts its turn within one or more emanations of a burning structure takes Thermic damage equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus. An ally that starts its turn within one or more emanations of a normal structure gains *Temporary Endurance* equal to your Thaumaturge Bonus.

#### Glowing Strikes < lvl 3 >
Your Guided Strike deals Thermic damage instead of your weapon’s normal damage type, and when you make a Guided Strike, you can spend 1 *Light*. If you do, your Attack Check is against the target’s $[PRS] instead of its AC, and it gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Burning 1.
**Deadly Success** :: Burning 2.
**Failure** :: 3 Thermic damage (add the value of your Mod Die as with a Successful Strike).
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Metalworks < lvl 4 >
Once each turn when you Pray, you can sacrifice *Temporary Endurance* to choose a manufactured metal object, such as a weapon or armor, that you can sense within your $[PER] Radius. You affect the object in one of the following ways. The amount you must sacrifice is listed beside the name of the effect.
:
**Refine (4)** The object is refined for 1 hour. A weapon’s DVs both increase by 1. An armor’s Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing Resistances increase by 2. The object also regains *Endurance* equal to your Thaumaturge Level. Refining the same object does not further increase its DVs or Resistances.
:
**Smelt (X)** The object glows red-hot. Each creature in physical contact with it takes X Thermic damage. Each creature holding or wearing the object that takes this damage must make a $[VIG] Save Check against your Spell DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: The creature may drop the object. If it doesn’t drop the object, its Attack and Skill Checks are Hindered 1 until the start of your next turn.
**Deadly Failure** :: The creature drops the object if it can. If it doesn’t drop the object, it has Disadvantage on Attack and Skill Checks until the start of your next turn.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

\column

**Warp (8)** The object warps, becoming drastically less effective. A weapon’s Deadly DV becomes equal to its DV. An armor’s Armor Bonus is reduced by 1. An object not being worn or carried takes Sonic damage equal to your Thaumaturge Level. Warping an object more than once only deals Sonic damage.

:
Additionally, during your Avatar Form you gain the following benefits.
:
* Your Miraculous Structures become solid steel.

* The amount of *Temporary Endurance* you gain from Hammerheart is doubled.

* You can use a Metalwork once on each of your turns by spending 1 $[AP] in addition to the required *Temporary Endurance*.

#### Forgemaster < lvl 7 >
Your Metalworks gain additional effects, described below.
:
* **Refine** The DV bonuses, Resistances, and regained *Endurance* are all doubled.

* **Smelt** You can target two objects.

* **Warp** Attack Checks made with a warped weapon are Hindered 1, a warped armor’s Resistances are halved, and Sonic damage dealt is doubled.

:
Also, during your Avatar Form any hostile creature that starts its turn in the emanation of your Miraculous Structures during your Raise Pillars miracle must make a $[PRS] Save Check against a DC of 10 plus your $[PRS] points.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Burning 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: Burning 2.
**Success** :: Nothing.
**Complete Success** :: The creature is Immune to this effect for 24 hours.
}}

#### Bishop of the World Forge < lvl 10 >
When you inflict the Burning condition on a creature, the level of that condition increases by 1, or 2 during your Avatar Form.

When you roll to gain *Temporary Endurance* with your Hammerheart facet, you can replace the value rolled with your Thaumaturge Bonus if it is lower.

{{wide,text-align:center

## Troubadour
Masters of the arts, Troubadours rouse the world with beauty beyond measure, evoking wonder with their craft.

}}

{{classTable,wide
##### Troubadour Class Table
|       | Troubadour  |                                              | Art     |        | Max Spell |
| Level | Bonus       | Facets                                       | Die     | Wonder | Level     |
|:-----:|:--:|:------------------------------------------------------|:-------:|:------:|:---------:|
|  1st  | +2 | Artistic Medium, Favored Genre, Artist’s Light        |   1d6   |   —    |    —      |
|  2nd  | +2 | Practice Drills, Wondercraft                          |   1d6   |   2    |    1st    |
<!--|  3rd  | +3 | Dedication                                            |   1d6   |   3    |    1st    |-->
}}

{{left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of an Alignment of your choice.

#### Endurance Points
You start with 20 *Endurance* and gain 10 per level.

#### Training
**Actions**      :: You can take Supernatural Actions.

**Armor**        :: You can add your Troubadour Bonus to your AC while wearing Light or Medium armor.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll $[PER] and $[PRS] Save Checks in Tandem with your Art Die.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[PRS] and Art Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Your $[PRS] Die is your Spell Mod Die. Checks you make with your Spell Mod Die count as $[PRS] Checks.

**Weapons**       :: You can wield any weapon that isn’t a melee weapon with the Two-Handed property.
}}

### Artistic Medium < lvl 1 >
Select your Artistic Medium from those listed at the end of the class description. Your Artistic Medium is the method through which you create. Your Artistic Medium grants you facets when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Favored Genre < lvl 1 >
Every artist has their preference, a style that deeply influences their work. Choose your Favored Genre from those listed after the class description. You gain benefits based on your choice, and you gain additional benefits at 5th and 8th level.

Each time you gain a Troubadour level, you swap your Favored Genre.

### Artist’s Light < lvl 1 >
As a practitioner of the arts, you can inspire rage and awe in equal measure. You gain the **Confound** and **Inspire** Actions, described below.

\column

##### Confound
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Move, Hostile
**Cost** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Trigger** :: A Hostile creature within your $[PER] Radius that can sense you makes a Check.
}}

Distracting the creature with a mocking display, you roll your Art Die and subtract it from the result of the triggering Check. If you aren’t Prone and have at least 2 *Movement*, you can also move a number of spaces up to your $[AGI] Points.

You can Confound a number of times equal to your $[INT] Points plus 1 (minimum 1). You regain all expended uses when you finish a Rest.
:
##### Inspire
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Move
**Cost** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Trigger** :: An Ally within your $[PER] Radius that can sense you makes a Check.
}}

Using your talents to create a moment of beauty, the Ally gains *Temporary Endurance* equal to your $[PRS] Points and may roll the triggering Check in Tandem with your Art Die. If you aren’t Prone and have at least 2 *Movement*, you can also move a number of spaces up to your $[AGI] Points.

You can Inspire a number of times equal to your $[VIG] Points plus 1 (minimum 1). You regain all expended uses when you finish a Rest.

### Practice Drills < lvl 2 >
Mastery of the basics is essential to any craft. You can spend 10 minutes doing practice drills to keep your mind sharp. Afterward, you gain a number of temporary *Radiance* equal to half your Troubadour Bonus that last until you finish a Rest. Once you use this facet, you must finish a Rest before you can use it again.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Wondercraft < lvl 2 >
Your art is so powerful it moves the world to imitate, allowing you to cast spells from the ***Wonder*** you create. You start with a maximum of 2 *Wonder*, and your maximum increases at certain Troubadour Levels, as shown in the Wonder column of the Troubadour Class Table. You regain expended *Wonder* when you finish a Rest.

#### Preparing spells
Each Long Rest, you can Prepare a number of spells equal to your Troubadour Bonus plus your $[PRS] Points, allowing you to cast those spells until your next Long Rest. Spells Prepared this way must have a level no higher than your Max Spell Level, as shown in the Troubadour Class Table. The types of spells you can Prepare are determined by your Favored Genre.

#### Casting Spells
Through your Artistic Medium, you create art, and the Prismos reacts. You gain the **Evoke Wonder** action, described below.
:
##### Evoke Wonder
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Move, Hostile
**Cost** :: $[SL] *Wonder*, $[AP] in spell’s Cast Time
**Requirements** :: You haven’t Evoked Wonder this turn
}}

You create art to cast one of your mantras or Prepared evocations at a level up to your Max Spell Level.

<!--### Dedication < lvl 3 >
You throw everything you are into your art. When you Inspire, you can spend 1 *Light*. If you do, the triggering Check is Empowered.-->

## Favored Genres
To create is to feel. Your Favored Genre represents those feelings you resonant with most strongly.

### Comedy 
There is no ailment not somewhat lessened by a laugh.
:
**1st Level** When you Inspire a creature, the *Temporary Endurance* it gains increases by twice your Troubadour Bonus.
:
You also gain a level of Lunar Alignment. 
:
**2nd Level** You can Prepare Flame, Pneuma, Mordant, Lunar, and Platinum Evocations.

### Tragedy 
Through sorrow, we are reminded what is truly important.
:
**1st Level** When you Confound a creature, it is Numbed 1 for 1 minute.
:
You also gain a level of Pneuma Alignment. 
:
**2nd Level** You can Prepare Pneuma, Velvet, Infernal, Lunar, and Platinum Evocations.

\column

### Romance
There is nothing so intoxicating as the scent of blooming love.
:
**1st Level** When you Inspire a creature, any player not playing the create may choose to spend one of their *Radiance* on the triggering Check. If they do, they gain Temporary Endurance equal to twice your $[PRS] Points.
:
You also gain a level of Velvet Alignment. 
:
**2nd Level** You can Prepare Flame, Frost, Soma, Velvet, and Platinum Evocations.

## Artistic Mediums
Few things move a crowd to action like a well timed art piece. The Artistic Medium of a Troubadour is platform from which they change the Prism.

### Dance
Mobile masters of the stage, Troubadours of Dance weave through battle like they been rehearsing for years.

#### Stagework < lvl 1 >
The Prismos itself becomes your stage crew, causing thin strings of Platinum Light to hoist you and your props. When you throw a Thrown weapon as part of an Attack Check, the weapon returns to you immediately afterward, so long as the target was within your $[PER] Radius.

You can also move in any direction while Jumping, instead of only a straight line. When you fall, subtract your Troubadour Bonus from the number of spaces you fell before calculating damage.

#### Dance Partner < lvl 1 >
When you end your turn in a space adjacent to an Ally, if you took a Move Action that turn they become your **Dance Partner** until the start of your turn. Once before then, you can Inspire one of your Dance Partner's Attack Checks without expending a use, and doing so only costs 1 $[AP].

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page


<!--{{wide,text-align:center

## Vampyre

Blasphemous creatures of the night, Vampyres strike fear and sow death wherever they roost.

}}


{{classTable,wide
##### Vampyre Table
|       | Vampyre   |                                              | Sanguine |         | Blood     | Max Spell |
| Level | Bonus     | Facets                                       | Die      | Blood   | Powers    | Level     |
|:-----:|:--:|:----------------------------------------------------|:--------:|:-------:|:---------:|:---------:|
|  1st  | +2 | Obsession, Vampyrism, Spider Climb                  |   1d6    |    4    |     —     |     —     |
|  2nd  | +2 | Blood Powers, Bloodline                             |   1d6    |    5    |     2     |    1st    |
|  3rd  | +3 | Profane Metamorphosis                               |   1d6    |    6    |     2     |    1st    |
|  4th  | +3 | Obsession Facet, Grave Strength, Adventurer Facet   |   1d8    |    7    |     2     |    1st    |
|  5th  | +4 | Vampyre Spawn, Bloodline Facet                      |   1d8    |    8    |     4     |    2nd    |
|  6th  | +4 | Vampyric Charm                                      |   1d8    |    9    |     4     |    2nd    |
|  7th  | +5 | Obsession Facet, Potent Metamorph, Adventurer Facet |   1d10   |    12   |     4     |    2nd    |
|  8th  | +5 | Dark Tenacity, Bloodline Facet                      |   1d10   |    14   |     6     |    3rd    |
|  9th  | +6 | Lord of the Night                                   |   1d10   |    16   |     6     |    3rd    |
|  10th | +6 | Obsession Facet, Eclipse, Paragon Facet             |   1d12   |    18   |     6     |    3rd    |
}}

{{descriptive,text-align:left
#### Alignment
You gain a level of Infernal Alignment.

#### Endurance Points
You start with 26 *Endurance* and gain 16 per level.

#### Training

**Armor**        :: You can add your Vampyre Bonus to your AC while wearing Light or Medium armor.

**Save Checks**  :: You roll your Sanguine Die in Tandem with your Attribute Die for $[VIG], $[AGI], and $[PER] Save Checks.

**Skill Checks** :: When you make a Basic Skill Check with your $[VIG], $[AGI], or $[PER] and Sanguine Die in Tandem, it is Empowered.

**Spells**       :: Your Sanguine Die is your Spell Mod Die.
}}

### Bloodline < lvl 1 >
Fractured memories of your prior life resurface. Where once was passion, in the dark now lies obsession. At 1st level, select your Obsession from those listed after the class description. It grants you features when you select it, and again at 4th, 7th, and 10th level.

### Vampyrism < lvl 1 >
As a newborn of the Cursed Ones, you have great strength and ability, though not without cost.

#### Blood
Through ***Blood***, there is power. You start with a maximum of 4 *Blood*, and your maximum increases each time you gain a Vampyre level, as shown in the Blood column of the Vampyre table. You regain expended *Blood* when you finish a Rest.

You can expend *Blood* to use various Vampyre facets such as **Regeneration**, described further below.

\column

#### Bloodlust
You gain no sustenance from normal food, and you do not need water. You must drink fresh blood in place of eating food to sustain yourself.

#### Supernatural Physique
The corrupted blood in your veins gives you supernatural ability. You gain the benefits below.
:
* ***Attribute Bonus.*** Your $[VIG], $[AGI], and $[PER] Points gain a +1 bonus. This bonus can surpass your Attribute Point Cap.

* ***Cursed.*** Your Creature Type is Obluminous Undead.

* ***Immortal.*** You don't need to breathe, and you don't age.

* ***Night Vision.*** You have Night Vision.

* ***Unarmed Strikes.*** When you make Unarmed Strike, the DV equals half your Vampyre Bonus, and the Deadly DV is your Vampyre Bonus.

:

#### Regeneration
When you start your turn with at least 1 *Endurance*, or when you Succeed on a Survival Save, you can expend *Blood* up to your Vampyre Bonus. You regain *Endurance* equal to your Vampyre Bonus times the *Blood* expended plus one roll of your Sanguine Die. If you use this facet while Enfeebled, the *Endurance* you regain is halved. When you take Divine damage, you can’t use this facet at the start of your next turn.

#### Vampyric Fangs
Your teeth are adapted for extracting the dark ichor. You gain the **Bite** action, described below.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--##### Bite
**Categories** :: Concentrate
**Cost** :: 3 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You aren’t Restrained.

Make an Unarmed Strike against a creature that has blood and is Restrained, Paralyzed, or Seized, rolling your Sanguine Die in Tandem with the Mod Die for your Attack Check. Your Strike deals Piercing damage instead of Crushing and gains additional effects if the target takes at least 1 Piercing damage. You regain *Endurance* equal to the Somatic damage dealt.

When you Bite a creature, you drink half a pound of its blood. You can also Bite a willing creature without making an Attack Check, and you can choose to deal 1 Piercing damage instead of the normal amount.

:
{{left
**Success** :: Somatic damage equal to your Vampyre Level. If the target’s size is Small or larger, you regain one expended *Blood*.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Somatic damage equal to your Vampyre Bonus.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Platinum Anathema
The sun is anathema to your existence. While in direct sunlight, you have the **Enfeebled** Condition, defined below.
:
##### Enfeebled
Your $[PER] and $[PRS] Checks are Weakened. If your head and body aren’t fully covered with clothing, armor, or something else to block the sun, you have Disadvantage on all Attack and Skill Checks, and you take Divine damage equal to your Vampyre Level at the start of your turn (or once every 5 seconds).

#### Obluminance
Anytime you gain a *Radiance*, it becomes an ***Obluminance***. You can’t spend *Obluminance* for anything that requires a *Radiance*, but you can spend it to do one of the following.
:
* Force any Check you (as a player) are aware of to be made at Disadvantage.

* Weaken any Check made with Disadvantage, including one you spent *Obluminance* to force Disadvantage on.

* Use a Hobby or Profession for any Skill Check, regardless of how relevant it would be.

* Deal bonus damage on a Weapon Attack equal to the weapon's Deadly DV.

\column

### Spider Climb < lvl 1 >
You cling to dark corners, descending from the rafters in the dead of night. Your normal *Movement* can also be used as *Climb Movement*, and you can move along vertical surfaces and upside down across ceilings without using your hands.

### Bloodline < lvl 2 >
Select your Bloodline from those listed after the class description. Your Bloodline represents the cursed strain of ichor that created you. It activates when you select it, granting you features at 2nd level and again at 5th and 8th level.

### Blood Powers < lvl 2 >
Your cursed blood grants you a myriad of powers, allowing you to cast spells.

#### Learning Spells
When you gain this facet, you learn one Blood or Infernal spell of your choice with a level no higher than your Max Spell Level. Your Bloodline also grants you a spell. You learn an additional Blood or Infernal spell and a spell from your Bloodline at certain Vampyre levels, as shown in the Blood Powers column of the Vampyre Class Table.

#### Casting Spells
You exact power from the obluminous ichor to cast fell curses. You gain the **Blood Cast** Action, described below.

##### Blood Cast
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** ::  2$[SL] *Blood*, $[AP] in spell’s Cast Time
**Requirements** :: You haven’t Blood Cast this turn.
}}

You cast one of your Blood Power spells at a level up to your Max Spell Level. If you are Enfeebled, your Max Spell Level is reduced by 1 for this Action.

### Profane Metamorphosis < lvl 3 >
You embody the many terrors of the dark. You gain the **Metamorph** action, described below.
:
##### Metamorph
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Hostile
**Cost** :: 2 $[AP], 3 *Blood* unless you haven’t Metamorphed since your last Rest.
**Requirements** :: You aren’t Enfeebled.
}}

You take the form of a **Bat** or a **Mist Cloud**. When you do, anything you are wearing or carrying that hasn’t been on your person for at least 24 hours falls to the ground, unless it is small enough to hold in your new form and that form is capable of doing so.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--During your Metamorph, you are considered unarmored and can add your Vampyre Level to your AC. Your Metamorph lasts a number of hours equal to half your Vampyre Level, or until you spend 2 $[AP] to end it. It ends immediately when you drop to 0 *Endurance*. The facets for each form are described below.
:
###### Bat
You become a cursed creature of ill omen, the bat. This form follows the rules below.

:

* ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* becomes *Fly Movement*, and you gain a normal *Movement* of 1.

* ***Size.*** You can’t speak, and your Size is Tiny.

* ***Unarmed Strikes.*** Your Unarmed Strikes deal no Crushing or Piercing damage.

* ***Unrestricted Bite.*** You can Bite creatures even if they aren’t Restrained, Paralyzed, or Seized.

:
###### Mist Cloud
Your form diffuses into a baleful mist. This form follows the rules below.

:

* ***Falling.*** If you would begin falling, you instead descend one space. You descend another space at the start of your turn (or one space every 5 seconds).

* ***Formless.*** You can enter spaces occupied by other creatures and stop there. You can move through any opening air could pass through, though you can’t pass through water.

* ***Immunity.*** You have Immunity against the Burning, Immobilized, Prone, Restrained, and Seized conditions.

* ***Movement.*** Your *Movement* becomes 4 and can’t be changed.

* ***Resistance.*** You have Resistance equal to your Vampyre Level to all damage except Divine.

* ***Weightless.*** You are weightless, can’t speak, and can only spend $[AP] to Stride or end your Metamorph (you can’t manipulate objects).

:
### Adventurer Facet < lvl 4 >
You gain one Adventurer Facet for which you meet the prerequisites. You gain an additional Adventurer Facet at 7th level.

\column

### Grave Strength < lvl 4 >
The deeper you drink, the stronger you grow. You gain the benefits below.

:

* ***Empowered Saves.*** When you would suffer a Failure on a $[VIG], $[AGI], or $[PRS] Save Check, you can expend one *Blood* to Empower the Check.

* ***Extra Attribute Point.*** You gain 1 Attribute Point.

* ***Potent Regeneration.*** When you use Regeneration, the *Endurance* regained now equals twice your Vampyre Bonus times the *Blood* spent, plus one roll of your Sanguine Die in Tandem with your $[VIG] Die.

* ***Potent Thirst.*** When you drop a Small or Larger creature to 0 *Endurance* by Biting them, you regain an additional *Blood*.

* ***Profane Resistance.*** You have Profane Resistance equal to your Vampyre Bonus.

### Vampyre Spawn < lvl 5 >
The bloodline is yours to grow. If you kill a Sapient at night with damage from Biting them, you can bury their corpse at least one foot deep in the ground before sunrise. If you do, the creature rises as your **Vampyre Spawn** at dusk the following day.

You can only maintain control over one Vampyre Spawn. If you create another one, your previous Vampyre Spawn loses its Bloodbound facet, becoming autonomous under the PM’s control.
:
Your Vampyre Spawn uses the statistics below.

#### Vampyre Spawn
*Undead (Size of the killed creature), level equal to your Vampyre Bonus*

::

|VIG|AGI|PER|INT|PRS|
|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|:-:|
|2 (d4)|2 (d4)|2 (d4)|0|0|

::
{{left
**Armor Class** :: Half the spawn’s level plus 4
**Endurance** :: 12 times the spawn’s level plus 10
:
**Movement** :: 6
**Perception Radius** :: 8
:
**Resistances** :: Profane equal to its level
:
**Professions** :: Those it had in life
**Hobbies** :: Those it had in life
**Languages** :: Those it knew in life
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--##### Traits
**Bloodbound** The spawn is bound to your will, using the following rules.
:
* ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the spawn, but you roll its Checks.

* **$[AP] Maximum.** The spawn’s $[AP] Maximum is 3.

* ***Obedient.*** It has a supernatural understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required) and will follow them to the best of its ability.

:
**Regeneration** If the spawn has at least 1 *Endurance* and isn’t Enfeebled, it regains *Endurance* equal to its level at the start of its turn. If the spawn takes Divine damage, this facet doesn’t function at the start of its next turn.
:
**Vampyre Weaknesses** The spawn has your Bloodlust and Platinum Anathema facets, as well as the anathema from your Bloodline.
:
##### Weapons
**Claws (d4 Mod Die)** half level plus 1 / level plus 2 Slashing
:
##### Actions
**Bite (2 $[AP])** The spawn Strikes a creature that has blood and is Restrained, Paralyzed, or Seized. The Strike deals Piercing damage instead of Crushing or Slashing, and if the target takes at least 1 Piercing damage, they take additional Somatic damage equal to the spawn's level on a Success, or twice its level on a Deadly Success. The spawn regains *Endurance* equal to the Somatic damage dealt.

### Vampyric Charm < lvl 6 >
Blood is a powerful aphrodisiac. You learn the **Enthrall** spell, described below.

##### Entrhall
*Blood Arcanum, 2nd-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Varies
**Components** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: 24 hours

Make a Spell Attack Check against the $[PRS] of a Sapient you can sense within your $[PER] Radius. If the target can’t hear you, your Attack Check is a Complete Failure.

:

{{left
**Success** :: The target regards you as a dear friend. They respond to your requests or actions as favorably as they can, and are willing to be Bitten.
**Deadly Success** :: The target sees you as a divine entity they would gladly lay down their life for. They takes no issue with any of your actions and will follow your requests to the best of their ability.
**Failure** :: The target is Dulled 5 for 1 minute. If you Bite them during that time, they are considered willing and will have no memory of it afterward.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

**Exalted (3rd)** You can target an additional creature.

### Potent Metamorph < lvl 7 >
Terrible and elusive, your form is evershifting. When you Metamorph, you can spend 1 *Blood* to reduce the $[AP] Cost by 1. If you do, you can become a **Bat Swarm**, described below.
:
###### Bat Swarm
:
* ***Bat Traits.*** This form has the traits of your Bat Metamorph.

* ***Immunity.*** You have Immunity against the Immobilized, Paralyzed, Prone, Restrained, Seized and Stunned conditions.

* ***Quick Bite.*** Biting costs 1 less $[AP].

* ***Resistance.*** You have Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing Resistance equal to your Vampyre Level.

* ***Sustained.*** This form lasts for 1 minute. You must Sustain it at the start of your turn or it ends early. Sustaining this form only costs 1 $[AP].

* ***Swarm.*** You can enter spaces occupied by other creatures and stop there.

### Dark Tenacity < lvl 8 >
When you drop to 0 *Endurance*, you can Metamorph into a Mist Cloud and Stride without spending any $[AP] or *Blood.* If you do, your Survival Save is automatically a Complete Success, and this Metamorph doesn’t end for being at 0 *Endurance*.

If you are still at 0 *Endurance* when this Metamorph ends, but have not died, you are Unconscious and lose the Dying condition. Once you Metamorph this way, you must finish a Long Rest before you can do so again.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--### Lord of the Night < lvl 9 >
Authority is your Bloodright. You gain the benefits below.

* ***Empowered $[PRS] Saves.*** You roll your Sanguine Die in Tandem with your Attribute Die for $[PRS] Save Checks, and they are Empowered.

* ***Extra Attribute Point.*** You gain 1 Attribute Point.

* ***Lord's Authority.*** You can control two Vampyre Spawn at once, instead of just one.

### Eclipse < lvl 10 >
The world will know its place, if only for a brief moment. You gain the **Eclipse** Action, described below.
:
##### Eclipse
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Hostile
**Cost** :: 3 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: You are outside during the day.

With a wave of your hand, swirling ichor eclipses the Sun above you, bathing a 1-mile-radius around you in blood-red light. For 10 minutes, light emitted by the sun in the affected area is not considered sunlight. If you would roll your Sanguine Die while you can see the Eclipse, use the die’s maximum value instead of rolling.

When you Eclipse, you can spend any amount of *Blood* to extend the duration of the Eclipse by 10 minutes per *Blood* spent. Once you Eclipse, you must finish 2d4 Long Rests before you can do so again.

### Paragon Facet < lvl 10 >
You gain one Paragon Facet for which you meet the prerequisites.

## Obsessions

### Perfections
Skilled mortals blessed with the Eternal Thirst find they have an eternity to obsess over their craft.

#### Immortal Superiority < lvl 1 >
Your Obsession with Perfection grants you the benefits below.
:
* ***Potent Senses.*** While Enfeebled, your PER Checks aren’t Weakened.

* ***Skill Checks.*** When you make an $[AGI], $[PER], $[INT], or Trained Skill Check, you can spend 1 *Blood* to roll your Sanguine Die in place of one of the Mod Dice for the Check. If you do, the Check is Empowered.

* ***Extra Hobbies.*** You gain three additional Hobbies at Amateur rank.

* ***Weapons.*** You can wield any weapon with the Ranged or Precise property.

\column

#### Table Etiquette < lvl 1 >
When you Unarmed Strike, your body has the Precise property (replacing its Deadly DV with the value rolled on your Mod Die).

Also, when you Seize or take a Move Action while you aren’t Enfeebled, you can spend 1 *Blood* in place of 1 $[AP] for that Action.

#### Vital Strike < lvl 1 >
Once each turn, you can spend 1 *Blood* when you make an Attack Check to roll your Sanguine Die in Tandem with the Mod Die for the Check.

#### Supernatural Reflexes < lvl 4 >
You gain 1 Attribute Point, which must be put into your $[PER] if able.

When you use Vital Strike, the Attack Check is Empowered.

You also gain the **Dodge** action, described below.
:
##### Dodge
**Categories** :: Concentrate, Move
**Cost** :: 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You aren’t Immobilized.
**Trigger** :: An Attack Check against you is Successful.

You react with supernatural speed. Roll your Sanguine Die and subtract it from the Attack Check, then redetermine the degree of success.

#### Terrifying Efficiency < lvl 7 >
You can spend *Blood* in place of $[AP] for the Precise weapon property. Each *Blood* you spend for this purpose counts as 2 $[AP].

#### Perfect Execution < lvl 10 >
You gain 1 Attribute Point, which must be put into your $[PER] if able.

When you would roll your Sanguine Die for a Save Check or Vital Strike while you aren’t Enfeebled, use the maximum value instead of rolling it.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

<!--## Bloodlines

### Ferrolord
Ferrolords bolster their form with the strength of iron. They can hide in plain sight even during the day by resembling cast iron statues; foreshadowing the fate of their prey.

#### Shapeless Anathema < lvl 2 >
While at least half your body is submerged in water, you are Enfeebled, and when you take Caustic damage, you can’t use Regeneration at the start of your next turn.

#### Ferrolord Blood Powers < lvl 2 >
When your Bloodline activates at level 2, and each time you gain new Blood Powers, you learn one World Arcana or Temporal Evocation.

#### Iron Resilience < lvl 2 >
When you Unarmed Strike, your body has the Heavy property, and both DVs increase by 1.

You also gain the **Ferromorph** Action, described below.
:
##### Ferromorph
{{left
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate
**Cost** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You aren’t Enfeebled.
**Trigger** :: You take Crushing, Piercing, or Slashing damage.
}}

You turn your flesh to iron at the point of impact. Roll your $[VIG] Die in Tandem with your Sanguine Die. The triggering damage is reduced by the higher roll. You can spend 1 *Blood* to further reduce the damage by your Vampyre Level.

#### Lineage of Iron < lvl 5 >
You and your Vampyre Spawwn can move through Treacherous Terrain made of earth, stone, or metal as though it wasn’t Treacherous. The Claws of your vampyre spawn also have the Heavy property, and their DV and Deadly DV both increase by 1.

You also gain the **Iron Glide** Action, described below.

\column

##### Iron Glide
**Categories** :: Supernatural, Concentrate, Move
**Cost** :: 1 *Blood*, 1 $[AP], Basic CAP
**Requirements** :: You aren’t Enfeebled

You Stride, and can move through solid earth, stone, and metal as if it was air and without destabilizing it. If you end your movement in a solid substance, you must Iron Glide again, otherwise you are ejected to the nearest unoccupied space and are Stunned 5.

Iron Gliding counts towards your CAP for Striding.

#### Statue Metamorph < lvl 5 >
When you Metamorph, you can become an **Iron Statue**, described below. While not Enfeebled, your Vampyre Spawn can spend 3 $[AP] to Metamorph into a statue as well, using their level instead of yours.
:
###### Iron Statue
* ***Covered.*** You aren’t Enfeebled while in direct Sunlight.

* ***Extended Duration.*** This form lasts a number of hours equal to twice your Vampyre Level, instead of half.

* ***Iron Form.*** Your skin becomes iron. You have Somatic Immunity and Arctic and Thermic Resistance 20. Anything you are wearing or carrying that has been on your person for at least 24 hours becomes iron as well, gaining the same benefits.

* ***Paralyzed.*** You are Paralyzed 1 for the duration of your Metamorph, and the level never reduces. At the end of your turn, you can choose to end your Metamorph (no $[AP] required).

* ***Resting.*** You can sleep and complete a long rest while in this form.

#### Courtyard Decor < lvl 8 >
You and your Vampyre Spawn have Crushing, Piercing, and Slashing Resistance equal to your Vampyre Bonus.

When you drop a creature’s *Endurance* to less than half their maximum with damage from Biting them, you can make a Spell Attack Check against their $[PRS].
:
{{left
**Deadly Success** :: The creature turns a stone statue (they become an object made of stone). You can feed on the statue as if it was still alive one time within the next week.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

Once you use this facet, you can’t again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you spend 3 *Blood* when you do.

![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page-->

{{wide,text-align:center

# Equipment

}}

{{wide

## Weapons

Listed in the table below are the details of all weapons available to PCs. Each row lists the weapon's name and the following information.

:
**Damage Value (DV).** The amount of damage dealt by the weapon on a Weapon Attack Check Success.
:
**Deadly Damage Value (Deadly DV).** The amount of damage dealt by the weapon on a Weapon Attack Check Deadly Success. A value of "Mod Die" means the Deadly DV equals the weapon's DV or the value rolled on your Mod Die for the Attack Check, whichever is higher.
:
**Damage Type.** The type or types of damage the weapon can deal. If multiple types are listed, you choose one each time you make a Successful Attack Check with the weapon.
:
**Requirements.** The Attribute Points required to properly wield the weapon. You have Disadvantage on any Weapon Attack Check you make with a weapon for which you don’t meet the listed requirements, or do not have the proper training to wield.
:
**Properties.** Properties affect how a weapon is used, and are defined below the table.

::
