{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Weapon Properties

Weapon properties drastically affect how a weapon is used. Take time to understand the properties of your chosen weapon and how you can use them to your advantage.
:
#### Ammo
Making an Attack Check with an Ammo weapon requires one piece of the ammunition listed in the property.

#### Blocking
While wielding a Blocking weapon, your AC increases by 1 if it’s Heavy, or 2 if it’s Massive. Blocking weapons are required to Block.

#### Bludgeoning
When you make an Attack Check with a Bludgeoning weapon, the target takes damage equal to the weapon's DV or the value of your Mod Die on a Failure, whichever is higher.

#### Brutal
When you make an Attack Check with a Brutal weapon, you can spend any amount of additional $[AP]. The Check Total is reduced by that amount, the weapon's DV increases by three time that amount, and its Deadly DV increases by four times that amount.

#### Close-up
When you make a Melee Attack Check with a Close-up weapon, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] to Step, before or after making the Attack Check. You can’t make Attack Checks with Close-up weapons while Immobilized, unless the target is touching you.

#### Deft
You can use $[AGI] instead of $[VIG] for Attack Checks made with Deft weapons, which have lower DVs but higher Deadly DVs. Wielding a Deft weapon requires at least 2 $[AGI] Points.

#### Drawn (X - Y)
When you take an Action to make an Attack Check with a Drawn weapon, you spend any amount of $[AP] listed in the range of the property, instead of the normal amount for that Action. The minimum you must spend is the amount normally required by the Action you’re taking, but for every $[AP] spent beyond the minimum, the below effects apply to your Attack Check.
:
* The weapon’s Deadly DV increases by 1.

* The value of your Mod Die increases by 1.

* The weapon’s short range increases by 2 spaces.

\column

You cannot make Reaction Strikes with Drawn weapons, and you have Disadvantage on Weapon Attack Checks with them if there is a Hostile creature within 1 space of you.

#### Heavy
Wielding a Heavy weapon requires at least 2 $[VIG] Points.

#### Light
When you Strike with a Light weapon during your turn, you can reduce the $[AP] cost of that Action by 1. You can do so once on each of your turns for each Light weapon you are wielding, but you can only use the Light property of one weapon per Action. Each time you do, your AC is reduced by 1 until the start of your next turn.

#### Massive
Any Action you take involving an Attack Check with a Massive weapon costs an additional 1 $[AP]. Wielding a Massive weapon also reduces your *Movement* by 2 spaces, and you can’t make Attack Checks with Massive weapons if you have less than 3 $[VIG] Points.

#### Nonlethal
Creatures reduced to 0 *Endurance* from damage dealt with a Nonlethal weapon do not die or lose *Vitality*. non-PC creatures are still knocked Unconscious.

#### Precise
You can use $[PER] instead of $[VIG] for Attack Checks made with Precise weapons, whose Deadly DV equals the weapon’s DV or the value rolled on your Mod Die, whichever is higher. When you make an Attack Check with a Precise weapon, you can spend any amount of additional $[AP]. For that Attack Check, the value of your Mod Die increases by the additional $[AP] spent. Wielding a Precise weapon requires at least 2 $[PER] Points.

#### Pulling
Once per turn, when you make a Successful Attack Check with a Pulling weapon, you can have the target make a $[VIG] or $[AGI] Save (their choice) against your Check Total.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: You move them 1 space to an unoccupied space adjacent to you.
**Deadly Failure** :: As a Failure, Prone.
}}

If your Attack Check was a Deadly Success, the Save Check gains the below additional effects.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: The target drops one object they are holding.
**Deadly Failure** :: You can catch the dropped object (no $[AP] required).
}}
![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Puncturing
Piercing damage dealt by Puncturing weapons ignores Piercing resistance granted by armor.

#### Pushing
Once per turn, when you make a Successful Attack Check with a Pushing weapon while you are not Immobilized, you can have the target make a $[VIG] or $[AGI] Save (their choice) against your Check Total.
:
**Failure** :: Buffeted 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: Buffeted 2 or Prone (your choice).

#### Ranged
Striking with a Ranged weapon has a basic CAP. When you make an Attack Check with a Ranged weapon, you must use $[PER] instead of $[VIG]. If the target is not within a number of spaces equal to the first number listed in the property, the Attack Check has Disadvantage. You cannot target anything beyond the second listed number of spaces. Wielding a Ranged weapon requires at least 2 $[PER] Points.

#### Reach
When you make an Attack Check with a Reach weapon, your target can be an additional space away from you.

#### Reload
After making an Attack Check with a Reload weapon, you can't do so again until you spend the $[AP] listed in the property to load a piece of ammunition into the weapon. Reloading a weapon is a Move and Manipulate Action.

#### Simple
Simple weapons don't need training to wield.

#### Sparklock
Making an Attack Check with a Sparklock weapon creates a thunderous sound audible out to 60 spaces, and requires 1g of Powdered Citrine per Attack Check.

#### Thrown
When you make an Attack Check with a Thrown weapon, you can choose to throw it, rolling either your $[VIG] or $[PER] Die in Tandem with your Mod Die for that Attack Check. If the target is not within a number of spaces equal to the first number listed in the property, the Attack Check has Disadvantage. You cannot target anything beyond the second listed number of spaces.

#### Two-handed
Wielding a Two-handed weapon requires two hands to be on the weapon while making an Attack Check.

\column

#### Versatile
You can wield a Versatile weapon with one hand or two. When wielded with two hands, use the DVs listed in parentheses.

## Armor
There are three types of armor in BtP: Light, Medium, and Heavy. Each has different effects when worn, described below.



### Light Armor
Your AC gains a bonus equal to your $[AGI] Points.

### Medium Armor
Your AC gains a bonus equal to your $[PER] Points.
:
**Resistance** You gain Slashing Resistance equal to half the armor’s AC value.
:
**Weighty** Your $[AGI] Save Checks are Hindered 1.

### Heavy Armor
Your AC gains a bonus equal to your $[VIG] Points.
:
**Resistance** You gain Slashing Resistance equal to your armor’s AC value and half as much Piercing Resistance.
:
**Restricted Movement** You *Movement* is reduced by 1 space and your $[AGI] Mod Die is reduced in size by one step.
:
**Heavy** Standing costs 2 $[AP].

### Armor Requirements
Armor has an amount of $[VIG] Points required to wear it effectively. If you don’t meet the $[VIG] Point requirement of armor you are wearing, all $[AGI] Checks you make are Weakened.

::

|  Type  | Requirement |
|:------:|:-----------:|
| Light  |    VIG 0    |
| Medium |    VIG 1    |
| Heavy  |    VIG 3    |

::

### Armor Quality
Armor has an AC value based on its type and quality, which represents the skill of its maker. While wearing armor, you add its AC value to your AC. The AC values for all armor types and qualities are shown in the table below.
:
##### Armor AC Values
:
|  Quality  | Light | Medium | Heavy |
|:---------:|:-----:|:------:|:-----:|
| Basic     |  3   |   4   |  6   |
| Adept     |  4   |   5   |  7   |
| Expert    |  5   |   6   |  8   |
| Master    |  6   |   7   |  9   |
| Legendary |  7   |   8   |  10  |

:



![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

### Slashing Vulnerability
While unarmored, you have Slashing Vulerability based on your maximum *Vitality*, as shown in the table below.

::
##### Unarmored Slashing Vulnerability
:
| Vitality Maximum | Slashing Vulnerability |
|:-:|:----:|
| 1 |	1d12 |
| 2 |	1d10 |
| 3 |	1d8  |
| 4 |	1d6  |
| 5 |	1d4  |
| 6 |	1d2  |

::

## Bulk
An object’s **Bulk Rank** measures how many of that object you can stow on your person. If an object has Bulk 1, you can only carry one of that item in your inventory. If an object has Bulk 2, you can only carry two of them, and so on to Bulk 5. Objects with Bulk 0 can’t be stowed on your person and must be carried with both hands. If an object has no Bulk, you can carry any number of them, subject to common sense.

Items of lower Bulk Rank can take up your inventory for items of higher Bulk Rank. For example, you could have three Bulk 2 items on your person, but if you do, you can’t stow a Bulk 1 item, as you can’t take up lower rank slots with a Bulk 1 item.

For each $[VIG] Point you have, you unlock additional inventory slots matching that number of points. So with 1 $[VIG] Point, you can carry an additional Bulk 1 item. With 2, you can carry two additional Bulk 2 items.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center

# Reading Spells & Maneuvers

}}

The following pages list all the spells and maneuvers that may be available to your PC, depending on your class. Each lists its name, type, level, cast or execution time, range, components or requirements, and duration, as well as their initial effects and Exalted effects. Spells and maneuvers that can be used as a Reaction will also list a trigger. If an attribute is listed as Varies, the effect text will explain it.

Each spell and maneuver uses the format below.

#### < Name >
*< Type > < Level >*
**Cast/Execution Time** :: X $[AP]
**Range** :: < Range /  Area >
**Components/Requirements** :: < Requirements >
**Duration** :: < Duration >

< Effect text >
:
**Exalted (level requirement)** < Exalted effects >

### $[SL] Level
Level is listed after type, below the name. The $[SL] symbol means to use the spell or maneuver's level. When you see a number beside the symbol, for example "2$[SL]", that equals the level multiplied by 2. Numbers are also sometimes added to the level, for example "$[SL] plus 1".

A creature that suffers a Failure against a Fire Spout spell will take 3$[SL] plus 2 Thermic damage. This means cast at 1st level, Fire Spout will deal (3 * 1) + 2 = 5 Thermic damage. At 2nd level, it will deal (3 * 2) + 2 = 8 Thermic damage.

### Type
Each spell and maneuver has a type that determines whether your PC can learn it based off their facets.

#### Arcanum Spell Types
The arcanum spell types are Fire, Ice, Mind, Bio, Scour, Arc, Blood, Moon, World, and Null. Creatures that draw light from within themselves are typically able to cast these spells.

#### Evocation Spell Types
The evocation spell types are Flame, Frost, Pneuma, Soma, Mordant, Velvet, Infernal, Lunar, Temporal, and Platinum. Creatures that wield light from external sources, such as dieties, are able to cast these spells.

#### Maneuver Types
The maneuver types are Feral, Martial, Instinctual, Tactical, and Psychological. Each of these types corresponds with an Attribute.

### Cast & Execution Time
The cast or execution time of most spells and maneuvers is measured in $[AP]. Spells with longer cast times measured in minutes can't be cast in a single turn, requiring 5 $[AP] every 5 seconds (each round of an Encounter) until the listed cast time has passed. Maneuvers with an execution time of Strike are done as part of the Strike Action.

Spells are cast as part of a separate Action, while Maneuvers with a cost time measured in $[AP] are considered Concentrate Actions.

#### Trigger
Reaction spells and maneuvers will list their trigger, which is the circumstance upon which you can use them. You can only take one Action in response to any single trigger.

### Range
Range is the effective area of a spell or maneuver. Most will measure their range in spaces, meaning the target can be no more than the listed spaces away from the creature using it. Many spells and maneuvers will also list an area of effect (see the Areas of Effect section for more details). Some special ranges are listed below.
{{left
**Touch** :: means the user must be able to physically touch the target.
**Self** :: will either only affect you, or will originiate from you if an area of effect is also listed.
**Vocal** :: can target any creature that can hear you.
**Strike Range** :: means within range of the weapon you are wielding.
}}
### Components & requirements
Most spells and maneuvers have one more components or requirements, some common ones are described below, but requirements can be many things.

#### Hands
One or two hands mean you must have that many hands free in order to cast the spell.

#### Spoken
A Spoken requirement mean you must vocalize some part of the action. If you can't speak, you can't use spells or maneuvers with a Spoken requirement.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Charged Gemstones
Some arcane spells require an amount of charge from a charged gemstone measured in grams (g). You must be holding the gemstone as you cast the spell, but the hand(s) used to hold it count as free for Hand requirements. After casting a spell with such a requirement, that much charge is depleted from the gemstone.

#### Powdered Gemstones
Some arcane spells require an amount of powdered gemstone, measured in grams (g). You must have the powdered gemstone in hand as you cast the spell, but the hand(s) used to hold it count as free for Hand requirements. After casting a spell with such a requirement, that much powdered gemstone is consumed.

### Duration
Duration explains how long the effect lasts. An **Instant** duration means the effect is performed upon use and is usually done afterward. A duration of 1 or more minutes creates a continuous effect that ends after the listed duration.

#### Sustained
Spells and maneuvers that list Sustained before their duration require you to take the Sustain Action on each of your turns to maintain the effect. If you don't, the effect ends at the end of your turn.

### Exalted
Exalted effects alter the way the spell or maneuver performs when used at higher levels. Exalted (+1) effects are applied once for each level above the base (listed) level. Exalted (+2) effects are applied once for every two levels above the base. Exalted effects that list a specific level are applied when the spell or maneuver is used at that level, in order from lowest to highest.

\column

{{background:darkblue,padding:1em,margin-bottom:0.5em
#### Note on Mod Dice
When a spell or maneuver requires you to make a Check, your Mod Die for the Check is your Spell or Maneuver Mod Die, unless otherwise stated. Your class will tell you what kind of Attribute Check your Spell Checks are considered (typically $[INT] or $[PRS]). A Maneuver Checks Attribute depends on its type, such as DC Checks for a Feral Maneuver counting as $[VIG].

This is important, because if you are Numbed 1 and you try to execute an Instinctual Maneuver, your Mod Die for Checks made as part of that maneuver will be reduced in size one step. An Arcanist that is Muddled 1 will similarly have their Spell Mod Die reduced for any Checks they make for their spells.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center
# Maneuvers
}}

### Feral
Feral maneuvers leverage the body's raw strength. Checks made using your Maneuver Mod Die for these maneuvers are considered $[VIG] Checks.

#### Brace
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Requirements** :: You are not Restrained
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You are targeted by an Attack Check.
}}
You prepare to take a hit. Before resolving the triggering Attack Check, you gain *Temporary Endurance* equal to one roll of your Mod Die plus 5$[SL].
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The triggering Attack Check is Hindered $[SL] minus 2.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Temporary Endurance you gain becomes one roll of your Mod Die plus 6$[SL].
}}

#### Brute Strength
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make a $[VIG] Skill Check.

You put everything you have into your effort. Your Skill Check is Empowered.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Your Skill Check is also Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered $[SL], instead of 1.
}}

#### Forceful Strike
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You are wielding a melee weapon that can deal Crushing or Slashing damage
**Duration** :: Instant
}}
You deliver a powerful overhead blow. You Strike, and your Attack Check is Bolstered 1. It also gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 4$[SL] Crushing damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 6$[SL] Crushing damage, and the target must Succeed on a $[VIG] Save against your Check Total, or fall Prone.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

\column

{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: 6$[SL] Crushing damage, and the target must Succeed on a $[VIG] Save against your Check Total, or fall Prone.
**Deadly Success** :: 9$[SL] Crushing damage, and the target has Disadvantage on their $[VIG] Save.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check Empowered.
}}

#### Hurl
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You are holding an object or creature at least one size smaller than you; You are not Restrained
**Duration** :: Instant
}}

You throw something with great force. Your Attack Check is against the target’s $[AGI] instead of its AC, and your Strike is a Ranged Attack using the held creature or object as your weapon, which deals Crushing damage, has a DV of 5$[SL] plus 4, a Deadly DV of 8$[SL] plus 6, and the Thrown (10$[SL]/20$[SL]) property.

If what you throw is a weapon with the Thrown property, it deals that weapon's damage type and you add the values of that weapon’s Thrown ranges and DVs to those listed in this maneuver.

If what you throw is a creature, it takes the same amount of damage as the target of your Strike, before Resistance.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You can use an object or creature your size as your weapon, which has a DV of 8 $[SL], a Deadly DV of 12 $[SL], and the Heavy and Thrown (5$[SL]/10$[SL]) properties.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can use an object or creature one size larger than you as your weapon, which has a DV of 9$[SL], a Deadly DV of 14$[SL], and the Massive, Pushing, and Thrown (3$[SL]/5$[SL]) properties.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You can use an object or creature two sizes larger than you as your weapon, which has a DV of 11$[SL], a Deadly DV of 17$[SL], and the Massive, Bludgeoning, Pushing, and Thrown (2$[SL]/3$[SL]) properties.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Savage Strike
*Feral Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

You throw all your strength into your Strike. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:

**Success** :: 5$[SL] Crushing damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 8$[SL] Crushing damage.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: The damage on any Success increases by $[SL].
**Exalted (+2)** :: The damage on a Deadly Success increases by an additional $[SL].
}}

### Martial
Martial maneuvers require training and precise timing. Checks made using your Maneuver Mod Die for these maneuvers are considered $[AGI] Checks.

#### Arresting Strike
*Martial Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: The target of your Strike is no more than one size larger than you.
**Duration** :: Instant
}}
You aim to hinder a creature’s movement with your Strike. Your Attack Check is against the target’s $[AGI] instead of its AC, and it gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Slowed 5 until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Immobilized until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Slowed $[SL] until the start of your turn.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: Immobilized until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, Sluggish $[SL] minus 1 until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Slowed 5 until the start of your turn.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: On a Success, the target is Sluggish $[SL] minus 2 until the start of your turn.
**Exalted (5th)** :: On any Success, you may have the target fall Prone. On a Complete Failure, the target is Slowed 3.
}}

\column

#### Concussive Strike
*Martial Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

You Strike, aiming to stun the target. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 2$[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned $[SL] minus 1.
**Deadly Success** :: 3$[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned $[SL].
**Failure** :: $[SL] Somatic damage.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Stunned $[SL] minus 2 on a Failure.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: 3$[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned $[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: 4$[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned $[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: 2$[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned $[SL] minus 2.
**Complete Failure** :: $[SL] Somatic damage, Stunned 1.
}}

#### Counter
*Martial Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: You are not Restrained
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: A creature you can sense within reach of your Strike suffers a Failure on an Attack Check against you.

Capitalizing on an enemy’s mistake, you Strike, targeting the creature that missed you, and your Attack Check is Bolstered $[SL].
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: If your Attack Check is a Success, the target has Disadvantage on Attack Checks against you until end of turn.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Finesse
*Martial Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make an $[AGI] Skill Check.

You move with practice and precision. Your Skill Check is Empowered.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Your Skill Check is also Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered $[SL], instead of 1.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Trip
*Martial Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: The target of your Strike is no more than one size larger than you.
**Duration** :: Instant
}}
You attempt to trip your opponent with your Strike. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Buffeted $[SL] plus 2, Prone.
**Deadly Success** :: Buffeted $[SL] plus 3, Prone.
**Failure** :: Buffeted $[SL] plus 1.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: On a Deadly Success, the target is Stunned $[SL] minus 2.
**Exalted (5th)** :: On a Success, the target is Stunned $[SL] minus 3.
}}

### Instinctual
Instinctual maneuvers turn raw sensory information into action. Checks made using your Maneuver Mod Die for these maneuvers are considered $[PER] Checks.

#### Buck
*Instinctual Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You are not Restrained
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You become Seized.

You attempt to buck off something that has Seized you. You Strike against the target’s $[VIG] or $[AGI] (its choice) instead of its AC. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: You are no longer Seized.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, and the target is Prone.
**Failure** :: The target is Buffeted 1.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: You are no longer Seized, the target is Prone and Buffeted $[SL] minus 2.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, but the target is Buffeted $[SL] minus 1.
**Failure** :: The target is Buffeted 1.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: The target is Stunned 1 on a Success, or 2 on a Deadly Success.
}}

\column

#### Dash
*Instinctual Maneuver, 1st-level*
{{left
**Execution Time** :: Reaction,  1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Requirements** :: You are not Immobilized or Prone and have at least 2 *Movement*
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make a Save Check or are the target of an Attack Check.
}}
You dash away from danger. After the Check resolves, you Step, moving up to 3$[SL] spaces instead of 1.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your AC increases by $[SL] minus 1 until the start of your turn.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You can move up to 4$[SL] spaces instead of 3$[SL].
}}

#### Grapple
*Instinctual Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You have an empty hand and are not Restrained.
**Duration** :: Instant

You attempt to latch onto your prey. You Unarmed Strike, and your Attack Check is against the creature’s $[VIG] or $[AGI] (its choice) instead of its AC. It gains additional effects.
:
**Success** :: The creature is Seized by you.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, Prone.
**Failure** :: Buffeted $[SL] until end of turn.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can Stride before making your Attack Check.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Lash
*Instinctual Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You are not Restrained
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You are targeted by a Melee Attack Check.

You lash out at your attacker. You Strike, targeting the creature that attacked you. Your Attack Check is Bolstered $[SL] and is against the target’s $[AGI] instead of its AC.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The triggering Attack Check is Hindered half $[SL].
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Sense
*Instinctual Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make a $[PER] Skill Check.

Your senses sharpen. Your Skill Check is Empowered.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Your Skill Check is also Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered $[SL], instead of 1.
}}

### Tactical
Tactical maneuvers require a razor sharp mind. Checks made using your Maneuver Mod Die for these maneuvers are considered $[INT] Checks.

#### Feint
*Tactical Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: You are wielding a melee weapon
**Duration** :: Instant

You flourish your weapon, attempting a feint. Make an Attack Check against the $[INT] of a creature you can sense within range of your Strike.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Attack Checks you make against the creature are Bolstered $[SL] until the end of you turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Attack Checks you make against the creature are Bolstered $[SL] plus 1 until the end of your turn.
**Failure** :: Your next Attack Check against the target costs 1 less $[AP].
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: On a Deadly Success, your Attack Checks also have Advantage until the end of your turn.
**Exalted (4th)** :: Your Attack Checks have Advantage on any Success.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You gain 2 temporary $[AP], which you can only use to Strike the target. They disappear at the end of your turn if unspent.
}}

#### Intuit
*Tactical Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make an $[INT] Skill Check.

You have an epiphany. Your Skill Check is Empowered.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Your Skill Check is also Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered $[SL], instead of 1.
}}

\column

#### Predict
*Tactical Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

You see the coming danger, and prepare to react. Attack Checks against you are Hindered $[SL] and your $[AGI] and $[PER] Save Checks are Bolstered $[SL] until the start of your turn.

{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The first Check Bolstered or Hindered by this maneuver also has Advantage or Disadvantage, respectively.
**Exalted (4th)** :: All Checks Bolstered or Hindered by this maneuver have Advantage or Disadvantage, respectively.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Duration of this maneuver becomes Sustained (1 $[AP]), 1 minute.
}}

#### Study
*Tactical Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Varies
**Range** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

You study a creature to learn its capabilities. Choose **Analyze** or **Analyzing Strike**, referencing the table below.

::

{{column-count:2
|                          |
|:------------------------:|
| AC                       |
| Highest Attribute value  |
| Lowest Attribute value   |

|                          |
|:------------------------:|
| Movements                |
| Resistances & Immunities |
| Vulnerabilities          |
}}

::

##### Analyze
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: $[PER] Radius

Choose a creature you can sense within range. Make a Tandem Skill Check with your Maneuver Mod Die and your $[INT] Mod Die against the $[PRS] of the creature. Your Skill Check is Empowered. If you target a non-Hostile creature, it also has Disadvantage.
:
{{left
**Success** :: You learn one of the creature's statistics in the table above.
**Deadly Success** :: You learn two such statistics.
**Failure** :: You learn one statistic of the PM's choice.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You Skill Check doesn't have Disadvantage against a non-Hostile creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

##### Analyzing Strike
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike Range

You Strike with planned movement, aiming to learn about your target. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:
**Success** :: You learn one of the creature's statistics in the table above.
**Deadly Success** :: You learn two such statistics.
**Failure** :: You learn one statistic of the PM's choice.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.

{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: You learn two of the creature's statistics in the table above.
**Deadly Success** :: You learn three such statistics.
**Failure** :: You learn one statistic.
**Complete Failure** :: You learn one statistic of the PM's choice.
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}


#### Surgical Strike
*Tactical Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

You Strike aiming for a specific weak spot. Your Attack Check has Disadvantage, but if you score any Success it becomes a Critical Success (you don't gain *Radiance* if you didn't roll a Natural 20).
:
{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: Your Attack Check doesn't have Disadvantage.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}



### Psychological
Psychological maneuvers can end a battle before it starts. Checks made using your Maneuver Mod Die for these maneuvers are considered $[PRS] Checks.

#### Deceive
*Psychological Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Vocal
**Requirements** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Instant

\column

You craft a lie that can’t be ignored. Choose a creature you can sense that can hear you. You make a Tandem Skill Check with your Maneuver Mod Die and your $[PRS] Mod Die against the $[INT] of the creature. Your Skill Check is Empowered. If you target a Hostile creature, it also has Disadvantage.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Stunned 1, and the creature is shaken by your lie, unsure if it is true.
**Complete Success** :: The creature believes your lie, and feels it must be acted upon immediately in manner determined by the PM.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
**Complete Failure** :: The creature doesn’t believe your lie, and may become Hostile.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You Skill Check doesn't have Disadvantage against a Hostile creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
}}

#### Distract
*Psychological Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Varies
**Range** :: $[PER] Radius
**Requirements** :: One Hand
**Duration** :: Instant

You create a distraction. Choose **Obvious** or **Subtle**.
:
##### Obvious
**Execution Time** :: 4 $[AP]

Choose a space you can sense within range. Each creature whose $[PER] Radius reaches that space makes an $[INT] Save Check against your DC Check. If an affected creature is not aware of any active threats, they will move toward the chosen space at their discretion.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Buffeted 2, Numbed 2, until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Failure** :: Buffeted 3, Numbed 3, until the start of your turn.
**Success** :: Buffeted 1, Numbed 1, until the start of your turn.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The Numbed level of any result except a Complete Success increases by 1.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Execution Time for this Maneuver becomes 2 $[AP].
}}
:
##### Subtle
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]

Make an Attack Check against the $[INT] of a creature you can sense within range. If the creature is not aware of any active threats, your Attack Check has Advantage.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{left
**Success** :: Buffeted 2, Numbed $[SL] plus 1 until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Buffeted 3, Numbed $[SL] plus 2 until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Buffeted 1, Numbed $[SL] until the start of your turn.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The Buffeted level of any result except a Complete Failure increases by 1.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Enrage
*Psychological Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Varies
**Range** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Instant

You cause a creature to seethe with rage. Choose **Taunt** or **Taunting Strike**.
:
##### Taunt
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Vocal

Your words inflame the ego of your target. Choose a creature you can sense that can hear you. You make a Tandem Skill Check with your Maneuver Mod Die and your $[PRS] Mod Die against the $[INT] of the creature. Your Skill Check is Empowered. If you target a non-Hostile creature, it also has Disadvantage.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Muddled 1 until the start of your turn, if the creature is Hostile it must make an Attack Check against you on its next turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Muddled 2 until the start of your turn, if the creature is Hostile it must make an Attack Check against you on its next turn. Otherwise, it becomes Hostile toward you.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
**Complete Failure** :: The target recognizes your taunt and becomes suspicious of its circumstances.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You Skill Check doesn't have Disadvantage against a non-Hostile creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
}}
:
##### Taunting Strike
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike range

You jeer at your target as you Strike.  Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Muddled 1 until the start of your turn, and the creature must make an Attack Check against you on its next turn.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, Numbed 1 until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
**Complete Failure** :: The target recognizes your taunt and becomes suspicious of its circumstances.
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: Your Attack Check is Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can Step after making your Attack Check.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Intimidate

*Psychological Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Varies
**Range** :: Varies
**Requirements** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Instant

You evoke fear from a creature. Choose **Frighten** or **Frightening Strike**.
:
##### Frighten
**Execution Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: $[PER] Radius

Your words instill mortal fear. Choose a creature you can sense that can hear you. You make a Tandem Skill Check with your Maneuver Mod Die and your $[PRS] Mod Die against the $[PRS] of the creature. Your Skill Check is Empowered. If you target a Hostile creature, it also has Disadvantage.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Panicked $[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: Panicked SL plus 1.
**Failure** :: Panicked half $[SL], rounded down.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: You Skill Check doesn't have Disadvantage against a Hostile creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
}}
:
##### Frightening Strike
**Execution Time** :: Strike
**Range** :: Strike Range

You Strike, your movements laced with cruelty. Your Attack Check gains additional effects.
:

**Success** :: Panicked $[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: Panicked $[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: Panicked half $[SL], rounded down.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You execute this maneuver twice.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The additional effects of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: 2 $[SL] Psychic damage, Panicked SL.
**Deadly Success** :: 3 $[SL] Psychic damage, Panicked $[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: $[SL] Psychic damage, Panicked half $[SL], rounded down.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: Your Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Provoke
*Psychological Maneuver, 1st-level*
**Execution Time** :: Reaction, 2 $[AP]
**Requirements** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You make a $[PRS] Skill Check.

Your action evokes strong emotion. Your Skill Check is Empowered.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Your Skill Check is also Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: Your Skill Check is Bolstered $[SL], instead of 1.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{wide,text-align:center
# Spells
}}

## Arcana
Spells crafted through the mortal manipulation of Light, referred to as Prisma by arcane practitioners.

### Fire

#### Arcane Fire
*Fire Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Varies

Using your arcane knowledge of Fire Prisma, you manipulate a minor amount of it in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Create Fire
You create a small flame emitting Low Brightness out to 5 spaces. The flame can levitate harmlessly in one of your free hands, or you can create it on any combustible material in a space you can sense within range. If you create the flame in your hand, it lasts while you Sustain this spell, up to 1 minute. Sustaining the spell this way only costs 1 $[AP]. If you create the fire on combustible material, you do not need to Sustain this spell to keep the fire burning; it burns until it runs out of fuel or blows out naturally.
:
##### Fire Shot
Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature within range. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Thermic damage dealt.

You can use Fire Shot as part of Sustaining the Create Fire effect of this spell. If you do, it costs an 3 $[AP] to Sustain instead of 1 $[AP], and the spell ends after you make the Attack Check.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Your level plus 5 Thermic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Thermic damage equal to your Class Bonus.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

##### Manipulate Fire Prisma
Choose a space you can sense within range that currently contains fire. You affect any mundane flames only emitting Low Brightness in that space in one of the ways below.
:
- You extinguish them. 

- You turn all spaces of Low Brightness adjacent to the flame into Full Brightness while you Sustain this spell, up to 1 minute.

\column

#### Thermic Ward
*Fire Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

You siphon Fire Prisma from your surroundings into a ward against heat. Touch a creature you can sense. For the duration, that creature has Thermic Resistance equal to twice your Class Bonus. If the target would take Arctic damage during that time, it instead takes no damage and the spell ends.

#### Embolden
*Fire Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, 2$[SL] g Charged Citrine
**Duration** :: Sustained (1 $[AP]), 1 minute
}}

You imbue a creature with Fire Prisma from the Charged Citrine. Touch a creature you can sense. That creature gains $[SL] temporary $[AP], which last for the duration and can only be used to cast a spell, Interact, Jump, Stride, or Strike. It can't be used to Prepare.

For the duration, the creature regains spent temporary $[AP] from this spell at the end of their turn.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell can be Sustained up to 10 minutes, instead of 1.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell no longer needs to be Sustained.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You can target two creatures within 3 spaces of you, instead of one creature you can touch.
}}

#### Fire Mote
*Fire Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, 2g Charged Citrine
**Duration** :: Sustained, 10 minutes

You extract stabilized Fire Prisma from the Charged Citrine. An orange mote of light appears at a space you can sense within range, emitting Low Brightness out to 10$[SL] spaces.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

When you cast this spell, you can make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within 5 spaces of the mote. Add your Mod Die to the Thermic damage dealt.

When you Sustain this spell, you can move the mote up to 5 spaces in any direction to a space you can sense and repeat the Attack Check. Alternatively, you can spend an additional 1 $[AP] to move it up to 10 spaces and detonate the mote, forcing each creature in a $[SL]-space emanation centered on the mote to make an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check. The spell then ends.

If you choose not to make the Attack Check or detonate the mote, this spell costs 1 less $[AP] to Sustain that turn, and you can move it up to 20 spaces. The spell ends early if you end your turn more than 5 spaces from the mote.
:
##### Attack Check
**Success** :: 7$[SL] Thermic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 11$[SL] Thermic damage.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
##### Detonation
**Failure** :: 5$[SL] plus 4 Thermic damage.
**Deadly Failure** :: 8$[SL] plus 6 Thermic damage.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell doesn't require the Charged Citrine to cast and its Duration becomes Sustained, 1 hour.
**Exalted (4th)** :: Each time you make the Attack Check, you can target half $[SL] different creatures using the same Attack Check. When you detonate the mote, the Thermic damage increases by $[SL] on any Failure.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Detonating the mote doesn't cost an extra $[AP].
}}

#### Fire Spout
*Fire Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 3-space line
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You concentrate Fire Prisma into a stream of Flame. Each creature in a 3-space line pointing in a direction you choose makes an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 3$[SL] plus 2 Thermic damage.
**Deadly Failure** :: 4$[SL] plus 3 Thermic damage, Burning $[SL].
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
}}

\column

**Exalted (3rd)** :: The line becomes 5 spaces, and a creature is Burning $[SL] minus 2 on a Failure.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The line becomes 25 spaces long, and you can form it along any continuous path, rather than it being straight.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You create a second line. Creature’s only make one Save Check if they are in both lines.

#### Prismfire Sigil
*Fire Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces, $[SL]-space circle
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You manipulate Fire Prisma into an arcane sigil on the ground that releases a flash fire. Choose a space you can sense within range. Each creature in a $[SL]-space circle centered on that point makes an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Burning half $[SL].
**Deadly Failure** :: Burning half $[SL] plus 1.
**Success** :: 3 Thermic damage, Numbed 1 until the start of your next turn.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: A creature becomes Burning 1 on a Success.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell's Cast Time becomes 1 $[AP] and the Range becomes 15 spaces.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell's Range becomes 20 spaces.
}}

#### Thermic Catapult
*Fire Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: $[SL] plus 3 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You release a flaming payload of Fire Prisma. Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within range. Add your Mod Die to the Thermic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 10$[SL] Thermic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 15$[SL] Thermic damage, Burning 1.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The creature is Burning 1 on a Success, or Burning 2 on a Deadly Success.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can target an additional, different creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: A creature is Burning 1 on a Failure, and the Burning level of every other result increases by 1.
}}

### Ice

#### Arcane Ice
*Ice Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 1 space
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Using your arcane knowledge of Ice Prisma, you manipulate a minor amount of it in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Freeze
You rapidly lower the temperature of a tiny object or single gallon of liquid in range to subfreezing temperatures. Liquids that freeze at the same temperature as water become ice. If you freeze a liquid this way, you can form the ice into crude shapes, but not into letters, accurate depictions of faces, or anything of similar detail. Objects and liquids affected by this spell warm up naturally over time.
:
##### Silver Dust
You release a frozen cloud in front of you. Choose two adjacent spaces within range. Creatures in those spaces make a $[VIG] Save against your DC Check. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Arctic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Your level plus 1 Arctic damage, Slowed 1 until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Failure** :: Additional Arctic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Slowed 2 until the start of your turn.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

##### Manipulate Ice Prisma
Choose a Large or smaller object you can sense within range. You affect the target in one of the following ways:
:
- You raise the temperature of the object to room temperature.

- You cool the object down to 70 degrees.

#### Artic Ward
*Ice Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

You siphon Ice Prisma from your surroundings into a ward against cold. Touch a creature you can sense. For the duration, that creature has Arctic Resistance equal to twice your Class Bonus. If the target would take Thermic damage during that time, it instead takes no damage and the spell ends.

\column

Exalted (4th) You gain 30$[SL] *Temporary Endurance*, instead of 25$[SL].

You launch a crystallized stake of Ice Prisma. Make an Attack Check against the $[PER] of a creature you can sense within range. Add your Mod Die to the Arctic damage dealt.
:

:

:

}}

You manipulate Ice Prisma into an arcane sigil at a space you can sense within range, covering the ground in a $[SL]-space circle centered on that space with ice.

###### Traits
**Familiar** ::The familiar is under your command using the following rules.

- ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the familiar, but you roll its Checks.

- ***$[AP] Maximum.*** The familiar’s $[AP] Maximum is 3.

- ***Initiative.*** If summoned during an encounter, it shares your Initiative and takes its turn after yours.

- ***Nonverbal.*** The familiar can’t speak, but has an arcane understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required), and will follow them to the best of its ability.

**Lightweight (1st & 2nd level only)** :: The familiar’s high jump can reach a height of 2 spaces, and its long jump has a maximum distance of 4 spaces.

**Size and Strength** :: The familiar's Size and $[VIG] are based on the level of this spell:

- **1st level** Tiny, -4 (-d8) $[VIG]; the familiar can manipulate objects weighing no more than half a pound.

- **2nd level** Small, -2 (-d4) $[VIG]; the familiar can manipulate objects weighing up to 10 pounds.

- **3rd level** Medium, 0 $[VIG].

- **4th level** Large, 2 (d4) $[VIG]; the familiar's *Movements* increase to 7 spaces.

- **5th level** Gigantic, 4 (d8) $[VIG]; the familiar's *Endurance* increases by 20 and its *Movements* increase to 8 spaces.

###### Weapons
**Freezing Blast ($[PER] Check)** :: half $[SL] plus 1 / $[SL] plus 2 Arctic, Freezing 1 on a Deadly Success.

### Mind

#### Disrupt
*Mind Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Roll your Spell Mod Die. A creature you can sense within range takes Psychic damage equal to the result plus your level.

#### Transference
*Mind Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 5 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: None
**Duration** :: Instant

Using your arcane knowledge of Mind Prisma, you manipulate a minor amount of it in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Relay
You psychically relay a message up to five seconds long to a number of creatures up to your Class Bonus that you can sense within range.
:
##### Sense Minds
You open your senses to ambient Mind Prisma, becoming aware of the position of each Ethereal and Sapient creature within an emanation out to your Class Bonus.

#### Alarming Ward
*Mind Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 1 minute
**Range** :: Self, Cylinder (3$[SL]-space radius, 3$[SL] spaces high)
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, $[SL]g Powdered Spinel
**Duration** :: 1 week
}}

You swirl the Powdered Spinel into the air, fixing the dust in place at the border of the Soulscape. For the duration, the arcane network of Mind Prisma created by the Powdered Spinel monitors the area within a cylinder. The cylinder’s radius and height can be up to 3$[SL] spaces, centered on the space where you cast this spell. Any time a creature with a soul enters the warded area, you receive a mental alert if you are within 10$[SL] miles of the area.

\column

While casting this spell, you can choose to create up to 5$[SL] seconds of words or sounds that the warded area repeats for 1 minute each time its activated.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 month.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 year, and you receive the alert if you are in the same facet of existance.
}}

#### Soul Seeker
*Mind Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 1 - 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 20 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Your incantation releases bolts of fast-moving Mind Prisma that seek their targets. For each $[AP] spent, target one creature you can sense within range. Each target takes 3$[SL] Psychic damage. You can target the same creature multiple times.
:
**Exalted (+2)** You can spend an additional 1 $[AP] when you cast this spell.

#### Summon Familiar
*Mind Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 5 $[AP]
**Range** :: 1 space
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, 2$[SL]g Charged Spinel
**Duration** :: Until Dispelled
}}
Extracting Mind Prisma from the Charged Spinel, you summon a spirit familiar resembling the ghostly apparition of a tiny creature of your choice. Each time you cast this spell, you can re-summon the same spirit, or release the previous spirit from your service and summon a new one.

During your turn, if you can sense your familiar, you can spend 1 $[AP] absorb it into a 2$[SL]g uncharged spinel that you are holding. While contained within the spinel, you can spend 1 $[AP] during your turn to release it in an unoccupied space adjacent to you.

Your familiar uses the stat block on the next page.
:

###### Traits
**Familiar** The familiar is under your command using the following rules.

* ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the familiar, but you roll its Checks.

* ***$[AP] Maximum.*** The familiar’s $[AP] Maximum is 3.

* ***Initiative.*** If summoned during an encounter, it shares your Initiative and takes its turn after yours.

* ***Nonverbal.*** The familiar can’t speak, but has an arcane understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required), and will follow them to the best of its ability.


**Ethereal** :: The familiar can’t physically interact with creatures or objects, takes no damage from falling, and any Crushing, Piercing, or Slashing damage it takes is halved. When it Jumps, it can move up to half its *Movement* vertically or horizontally, instead of the normal amount of spaces.

**Perfect Memory** :: The familiar remembers everything it hears and sees after your initial summoning. Even if it dies, it retains all memories it has from being in your service.

**Telepathic Bond** :: The familiar can psychically relay information to you while you are within its $[PER] Radius times $[SL]. The information it can relay is based on the level of this spell:

* **1st level and up** It can relay words and sounds.

* **2nd level and up** It can project still images of things it has seen.

* **4th level and up** It can project any memory it has acquired while summoned by you.

* **5th level** You can speak to the familiar telepathically while within range of its Telepathic Bond.

:
###### Actions
**Psychic Blast (2 $[AP])** :: The familiar glows faintly and makes an $[INT] Attack Check against the $[INT] of a creature it can sense within range of its Telepathic Bond.
:
**Success** :: Half $[SL] plus 1 Psychic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: $[SL] plus 2 Psychic damage.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Telekinesis (3rd level, 0 $[AP], Basic CAP)** ::The familiar Interacts, exerting telekinetic force to accomplish the action and using its $[INT] in place of its $[VIG] for any Skill Checks required.
:
}}

#### Thought Transmission
*Mind Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: 15 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, One Hand
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You sense a creature with whom you want to convey information.

You stream a ray of Mind Prisma directly to another creature you can sense within range. You convey up to 1 minute of information to the target, which can include words, memories with full sensory experience, and any abstract or solitary images or smells.

Alternatively, you can spend 10 minutes channeling information into a 1g or larger Charged Spinel. When you cast this spell with the Charged Spinel in your hand,  you can expend its charge to convey all 10 minutes of information to the target, who is then Muddled 1 until the end of their turn.

:
{{left
**Exalted (+2)** ::The range increases by 15 spaces and you can convey the same information to an additional creature you can sense within range.
}}

### Bio

#### Adrenalize
*Bio Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: 1 minute

You harness a minor amount of Bio Prisma for an adrenaline boost. Touch a creature you can sense. The target gains *Temporary Endurance* equal to your level plus 5 that lasts for the duration. While they have *Temporary Endurance* from this arcanum, they gain the benefits below.
:
* ***Speed.*** The creature’s *Movement* increases by half your Class Bonus.

* ***Force.*** When the creature makes a Successful Weapon Attack Check, the *Temporary Endurance* is sacrificed and the target of their Attack Check takes additional damage of a type dealt by the weapon equal to the amount sacrificed.

* ***Overdrive.*** When the target makes a $[VIG], $[AGI], or $[PER] Save or Skill Check, the Check is Bolstered 1. Any *Temporary Endurance* remaining after the Check resolves is sacrificed.

\column

#### Leap
*Bio Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Components** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Instant

You Jump, moving a number of spaces up to your Class Bonus plus 3 horizontally, and up to your Class Bonus vertically. Any crushing damage you take from falling after this Jump is reduced by your level.

#### Boost
*Bio Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 3 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, One Hand, 2$[SL]g Charged Tourmaline
**Duration** :: 1 minute
}}

You boost a creature’s speed with Bio Prisma from the Charged Tourmaline. Choose a creature you can sense within range. The target’s *Movement* increases by $[SL] plus 2.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The creature’s $[PER] Checks are Bolstered $[SL] minus 2.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 10 minutes.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 hour.
}}

#### Enhanced Strike
*Bio Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, One Hand
**Duration** :: Instant

You strengthen your body with Bio Prisma to make a fast and powerful strike with one of your limbs. Make an Attack Check against the $[PER] of a creature you can sense and touch. Add your Mod Die to the Crushing damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 8$[SL] plus 5 Crushing damage.
**Deadly Success** :: 12$[SL] plus 8 Crushing damage, Buffeted $[SL].
**Critical Success** :: As a Deadly Success, Bruised, Prone.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The creature is Buffeted $[SL] on a Success, or $[SL] plus 1 on a Deadly Success.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The creature is Numbed 1 on a Success, or Numbed 2 on a Deadly Success, until the start of your turn.
}}

#### Invigorate
*Bio Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 10 minutes

You imbue a creature with Bio Prisma to enhance their strength. Touch a creature you can sense. They gain 2$[SL] *Temporary Endurance*, and for the duration have the benefits below.
:
- $[VIG] ***Checks.*** Their $[VIG] Checks are Bolstered 1.

- ***Jump Distance.*** When it Jumps, the maximum distance of their High Jump increases by $[SL], and the maximum distance of their Long Jump increases by 2$[SL].

- ***Standing Jump.*** They can Long Jump $[SL] plus one spaces without moving before hand.

- ***Temporary Endurance.*** They gain 2$[SL] *Temporary Endurance* at the start of their turn.

:
{{left
**Exalted (+2)** :: The creature’s $[VIG] Checks are Bolstered an additional level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You can target an additional creature you can touch.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Range becomes 5 spaces, and you can target three creatures within range.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes Sustained, 1 hour.
}}

#### Wither
*Bio Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, $[SL]g Powdered Tourmaline
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute
}}

Scattering the Powered Tourmaline into the air, you siphon a creature’s Bio Prisma into the dust to wither their body. When you cast this spell and each time you Sustain it, make an Attack Check against the $[VIG] of a creature you can sense within range. Add your Mod Die to the Somatic damage dealt.
:

:

### Scour

#### Acid Mist
*Scour Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 1 space
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You disperse Scour Prisma into an acidic mist in front of you. Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within range. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Caustic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Caustic damage equal to your level.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Caustic damage equal to your Class Bonus. If the creature has eyes, it is Blinded until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Arcane Brine
*Scour Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Biting your tongue to conjure a minor amount of Scour Prisma, you summon briny water in one of the ways described below. The water is too salty to drink.
:
##### Collect
You fill a Medium or smaller object you can sense within range with brine. The object must be able to hold liquid.
:
##### Downpour
You summon a flash of salty rainfall in a cylinder 3 spaces high with a radius up to 3 spaces. The downpour douses mundane flames within the area and reduces the Dissolving level of creatures within it by 1.
:
##### Stream
You arc a stream of water through the air up to 15 spaces away. You may target a creature within that range. If it doesn't want to get wet it may make an $[AGI] Save against your DC Check. On a Failure, it is doused. The water reduces the Dissolving level of the creature by 2.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Acid Spout
*Scour Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Varies
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You concentrate Scour Prisma into a spout of acid that you can condense or disperse. Choose **Spray** or **Stream**.
:
##### Spray
Each creature in a $[SL] plus 1 space cone pointing from you in a direction you choose makes a $[PER] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Dissolving $[SL] plus 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: Dissolving $[SL] plus 2.
**Success** :: Dissolving half $[SL].
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The Dissolving level on any Failure increases by 1.
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Dissolving $[SL] on a Success, or Dissolving half $[SL] on a Complete Success.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Creatures in the cone take 2$[SL] Caustic damage before making their Save Check.
}}
:
##### Stream
Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within 5$[SL] spaces. Add your Mod Die to the Caustic damage dealt.

{{left
**Success** :: $[SL] plus 4 Caustic damage, Dissolving 2$[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: 2$[SL] plus 6 Caustic damage, Dissolving 2$[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success, Dissolving $[SL].
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The Dissolving level on a Success, Deadly Success, and Failure increase by 1.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can target an additional, different creature.
**Exalted (5th)** :: A creature is Dissolving $[SL] on a Complete Failure, and the Dissolving level for every other result increases by 1.
}}

#### Biting Rebuke
*Scour Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: You take damage from an Action of a creature you can sense within range.
}}

Using your pain, you guide Scour Prisma back to the attacker to sear their flesh. Make an Attack Check against the $[PER] of the triggering creature. On any result other than a Complete Failure, the creature takes Caustic damage equal to the value of your Mod Die.
:
**Success** :: Dissolving 2$[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: Dissolving 2$[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: Dissolving $[SL].
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The results of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: Dissolving 3$[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: Dissolving 3$[SL] plus 1.
**Failure** :: Dissolving 2$[SL].
**Complete Failure** :: Dissolving $[SL].
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can target another Hostile creature within range, using the same Attack check.
**Exalted (4th)** :: You can target a third Hostile creature.

#### Whirlpool
*Scour Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, 2$[SL]g Powdered Titanite
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute
}}

Mixing the Powdered Titanite with brine summoned from Scour Prisma, you launch a payload arcane water through the air. Choose a space you can sense within range. The water erupts into a violent whirlpool covering a 2-space radius, 3 space high cylinder centered on that space.

Spaces within the whirlpool are Treacherous Terrain, and any creature that takes a Move Action while within the whirlpool must spend an additional 1 $[AP] to do so. Any creature that takes a Move Action and attempts to move out of the whirlpool first makes a $[VIG] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: The creature is prevented from exiting and the remainder of its Action is wasted.
**Deadly Failure** :: The creature falls Prone and is moved to a random space within the whirlpool.
**Success** :: The creature can exit the whirlpool by spending 2$[SL] *Movement*.
**Complete Success** :: The creature can exit the whirlpool by spending $[SL] *Movement*
:
**Exalted (+1)** :: The radius of the whirlpool increases by 1 space.
**Exalted (2nd)** :: Creatures that start their turn in the whirlpool are moved 1d4 spaces clockwise per Exalted level.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The height of the whirlpool increases to 5 spaces.
}}

### Arc

#### Arc Wind
*Arc Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Varies
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Directing Arc Prisma from the air around you, you manipulate wind in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Ion Gale
You charge the air around you with Arc Prisma to create a sudden, sparking gale. Each creature in a 1-space emanation makes a $[PER] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** ::Galvanic damage equal to the value of your Mod Die for the DC Check, Move Actions don’t trigger Reactions from the creature until the start of your next turn.
**Deadly Failure** :: As a Success, additional Galvanic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Slowed 1 until the start of your turn.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}
:
##### Gust
You funnel wind into a gust covering a 5-space line pointed from you in a direction of your choice. Each creature in the gust makes a $[VIG] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Prone.
**Deadly Failure** :: Prone, and the creature is moved 1 space away from you along the gust.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Arcane Electricity
*Arc Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Using your arcane knowledge of Arc Prisma, you manipulate a minor amount of it in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Electrify
You release an arc of electricity. Make an Attack Check against the $[PER] of a creature you can sense within range, and another creature you can sense within 5 spaces of it. The second creature doesn’t need to be within range of the spell, and you use the same Check for both creatures. Add your Mod Die to the Galvanic damage dealt.

\column

{{left
**Success** :: Your level plus 1 Galvanic damage.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Galvanic damage equal to your Class Bonus.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}
:
##### Magnetize
Choose an unoccupied space you can sense within range. You flash a spinning mote of Arc Prisma in that space, creating a magnetic field. Any loose metal objects that are Small or Tiny within 2 spaces of the mote are pulled into its space. You can direct the field towards a creature within 1 space of the mote that is wearing metal armor or wielding a metal object. Make an Attack Check against that creature’s $[VIG]. Add your Mod Die to the Galvanic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Galvanic damage equal to your level, and the creature is pulled into the mote’s space. If the creature is not wearing metal armor, it can let go of its object, which moves instead.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Galvanic damage equal to your Class Bonus, and the creature or object is moved an additional space in the same direction. The creature also falls prone if it doesn’t let go of its object.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}
:
##### Manipulate Arc Prisma
Choose a Medium or smaller metal object or a creature you can sense within range. If you choose a creature, it makes a $[VIG] Save Check against your DC Check. If you choose an object, make a DC Check and note the result. The object becomes electrified until the start of your turn (or for 5 seconds), and each creature that touches it (including any currently doing so) until then makes a $[VIG] Save Check against the result of your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Stunned 2.
**Deadly Failure** :: Stunned 3.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Updraft
*Arc Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces, cylinder (4-space radius, 5 spaces high)
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

}}

You rapidly swirl a large amount of Arc Prisma into a violent updraft on the ground beneath the falling creature. The updraft is a 4-space radius cylinder that reaches 5 spaces high. Pulling on the Blood Prisma within a creature, you extract their life essence from their pores. Make an
