#### Manipulate Emotion
*Blood Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: Varies
**Components** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Varies

You speak words laced with Blood Prisma that can manipulate emotion in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Invoke Fear
Your words assault the resolve of a creature. Choose two creatures you can sense within 5 spaces of you that can hear you. Each of those creatures makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
**Failure** :: Panicked $[SL] plus 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: Panicked $[SL] plus 2.
**Success** :: Panicked half $[SL].
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You can target $[SL] creatures within 5 spaces of you that can hear you, instead of two.
:
##### Subdue Ego
Your words make the target vulnerable to manipulation. Make an Attack Check against the $[PRS] of a creature you are touching that can hear you. For 1 minute, any $[PRS] Skill Check made by a creature to influence the target are affected based on the result of your Attack Check.

Casting this option of the spell isn’t a Hostile Action, and isn’t noticeable unless observers are sensitive to the arcane.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Bolstered $[SL].
**Deadly Success** :: Bolstered $[SL], Advantage.
**Failure** :: Only the first Skill Check made within the duration is Bolstered $[SL].
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing, and the target knows you tried to manipulate their emotions.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: On a Success, $[PRS] Skill Checks made to influence the creature have Advantage. On a Deadly Success, they have Advantage and are Empowered.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 hour, and when you cast the spell you can choose specific creatures within 5 spaces of you to be the only ones whose Checks benefit from the spell.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 8 hours, and the results of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: Bolstered $[SL], Advantage, Empowered.
**Deadly Success** :: Result can’t be lower than a Success.
**Failure** :: Bolstered $[SL] and Advantage for 1 hour.
**Complete Failure** :: Only the first Skill Check made within the next minute is Bolstered $[SL].
}}

### Moon

#### Arcane Tricekry
*Moon Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Varies
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Aligning yourself with ambient Moon Prisma, you manipulate a minor amount of it in one of the ways described below.
:
##### Ephemeral Illusion
Choose a space you can sense within 10 spaces of you. That space is filled with a number of smells or sounds up to half your Class Bonus that each last up to 5 seconds. The sound can be anything, including words. Either option can cause significant discomfort, but not cause damage.

When you cast this option of the spell, the spoken requirement may be whispered, but you must be able to speak.
:
##### Overwhelm Intellect
Make an Attack Check against the $[INT] of a creature you can touch. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Cosmic damage dealt.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

{{left
**Success** :: Cosmic damage equal to your level, Muddled 1 until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Cosmic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Muddled 2 until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}
:
##### Manipulate Moon Prisma
Touching the ground beneath you, you experience a sped up replay of the events that occurred within a 1-space emanation around you, up to a number of hours ago equal to your Class Bonus. While experiencing the events, you hear no sound, but can make out the faces of people that come into view. Once during the replay, you can slow it down to normal speed, hearing sounds that passed through the emanation.  This slowed perspective lasts for a number of minutes up to your level.

If you use this option more than once before finishing a Rest, you become Muddled X until you finish a Rest, where X is the number of times you used this option since your last Rest.

#### Eavesdrop
*Moon Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 5 $[AP]
**Range** :: 20 spaces, 2-space-radius sphere
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 10 Minutes

You tune in to the Moon Prisma around you to eavesdrop on a conversation.  Choose a space you can sense within range. For the duration, you hear any words spoken by creatures in a 2-space-radius sphere centered on that space.

The spoken requirement of this spell may be whispered, but you must be able to speak.

#### Secret Script
*Moon Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: Varies, minimum 1 minute
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Two Hands, Ink made from 2$[SL]g of Powdered Sapphire
**Duration** :: 1 week
}}

Using the sapphire ink, you spend up to $[SL] hours writing or drawing on a suitable surface. Anything you write or draw is imbued with an illusion for the duration, which can alter your script in any number of ways below.

\column

* ***Gibberish.*** You make the script completely unintelligible.

* ***Handwriting.*** You change the handwriting of the script to that of a different creature, which you must observe a sample of while writing for this spell.

* ***Script Size.*** You change the script to any other of similar size.

* ***Language.*** You change the language to another which you know, including any cyphers you are trained in.

:
You and any creatures you choose that you can sense while writing for this spell can see through the illusion. You can also imbue the illusion with a spoken password, written pass code, or both. When the password is said allowed it removes the illusion for 10 minutes. When the pass code is written in the spot you designated for it, the illusion is permanently removed.

If this spell is dispelled, both the illusion and the original script disappear from the surface.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 month.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You can have the illusion make your script invisible.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes 1 year.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The spell lasts until dispelled.
}}

#### Sensory Illusion
*Moon Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 10 minutes

Weaving Moon Prisma into a cloud, you send it forth, creating a sensory illusion. Choose **Smell** or **Sound**. A 1-space radius cylinder that reaches 3 spaces high is filled with the illusion you designate. Creatures outside the cylinder don’t experience the illusion at all. Each time you Sustain this spell, you can adjust the illusion, and even choose a different mode, ending the previous mode.

The spoken requirement of this spell may be whispered, but you must be able to speak.

:
##### Smell
You create a smell within the cylinder. It can fill the entire cylinder or emanate from a specific space, getting stronger as a creature gets closer to it. It can’t deal damage, but can be pungent enough to cause discomfort.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

##### Sound
You create a sound within the cylinder. It can appear to come from any direction you choose, and last as long as you decide. It can’t be loud enough to deal damage or cause prolonged hearing loss, though it can be loud enough to cause discomfort. 
:
{{left
**Exalted (+2)** :: The radius of the cylinder increases by 2.
**Exalted (2nd)** :: You can create both kinds of illusions at once when  you cast or Sustain the spell.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Duration becomes Sustained, 8 hours. When you cast the spell, you may have the Duration become 10 minutes, without needing to be Sustained, though you can’t alter the illusion after casting if you do.
}}

### World

#### Arcane Hand
*World Cantrip*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, One Hand
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

Coating your hand in World Prisma, you conjure a duplicate of it made from arcanely stabilized sonic pressure in a space you can sense within range. Each time you Sustain the spell, you can move the hand up to 5 spaces.

You can use the arcane hand to Interact with objects within 1 space of it. $[VIG] and $[AGI] Checks made to interact with objects this way are made as if you were using your own hand. However, you gain no special ability to see objects you Interact with this way, so if the interaction requires a $[PER] Check, it is made from your space.

#### Stunning Stone
*World Cantrip*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands; a rock or rocky ground beneath you
**Duration** :: Instant
}}
You overload a palm-sized stone with World Prisma and launch it. Make an Attack Check against the AGI of a creature you can sense within range. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Crushing damage dealt.
:
**Success** :: Crushing damage equal to your level.
**Deadly Success** :: Crushing damage equal to your level, Sonic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Stunned 1.
**Failure** :: Nothing.

\column

#### Arcane Armor
*World Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: Reaction, $[AP] Varies
**Range** :: Self
**Components** :: Two Hands, Spoken; *Arcane Armor:* $[SL]g Powdered Emerald
**Duration** :: Varies
**Trigger** :: *Arcane Armor:* You become aware of a dangerous situation; *Sonic Shield:* You are targeted by an Attack Check.
}}

Reacting to danger, you craft World Prisma around you to defend yourself. Choose **Arcane Armor** or **Sonic Shield**.
:
##### Arcane Armor
You spend 1 $[AP] to quickly release a cloud of Powdered Emerald around you, creating an arcane suit of armor. For 8 hours, you gain the benefits below.
:
- ***Slashing Resistance.*** You have Slashing Resistance equal to half $[SL] plus 1 and no Slashing Vulnerability.

- ***Armor Class.*** Your AC equals $[SL] plus 3, your Class Bonus, and your $[INT] Points.

:
##### Sonic Shield
In response to being targeted by an Attack, you conjure a barrier of pressure. The barrier has a DV of 3, a Deadly DV of 4, and the Blocking property. You immediately Block, spending any amount of $[AP], and you can apply the Blocking Bonus to the triggering Attack Check.
:
{{left
**Exalted (2nd)** :: The barrier’s DV's both increase by 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The barrier gains the Heavy property.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The barrier gains the Massive property.
}}

#### Raise Pillar
*World Arcanum, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 2 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Two Hands, Spoken; natural ground at the target space
**Duration** :: Sustained, 10 minutes
}}

Shaping the World Prisma beneath your feet, you raise a pillar of earth. Choose a space you can sense within range. A pillar of earth 1 space wide and up to 5$[SL] spaces high rises at that space. If there is a Medium or smaller creature in that space, you can raise the pillar at a steady pace if they are willing, elevating them with the pillar. Otherwise, you can make an Attack Check against their $[AGI].

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

When you Sustain the spell, you can choose to have the pillar lower gently, placing any creature on it back at ground level and ending the spell. Otherwise, when you end your turn without Sustaining the spell, the pillar shatters, and any creature on it falls.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Prone, and the creature is elevated with the pillar. If the pillar collides with a ceiling, the creature takes 5$[SL] Crushing damage and the pillar shatters, causing the creature to fall.
**Deadly Success** :: As a Success, Stunned $[SL].
**Failure** :: Prone, and the creature moves to an adjacent space of their choice.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
}}
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: You can choose another space within range, creating a second pillar.

### Null

#### Dispel Prisma
*Null Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You siphon away all Prisma from your target, nullifying prismatic effects. Choose a creature or object you can sense within range. Any spells or ongoing arcane or supernatural effects level $[SL] or lower affecting the target end for them. The Burning, Freezing, Morphing, and Possessed Conditions also end for the target. If the target is a level $[SL] or lower arcane or supernatural item, it loses its arcane or supernatural properties for 24$[SL] hours.

The size of object you are able to target depends on the spell’s level, as shown below.
::
|Level|Size|
|:-:|:-:|
|1|Medium|
|2|Large|
|3|Gigantic|
|4|Enormous|
|5|Gargantuan|

\column

#### Null Ward
*Null Arcanum, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: Reaction, 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant
**Trigger** :: A creature you can sense within range makes a Save Check against a spell.

You create a void between the spell and $[SL] creatures you can sense within range making the Save Check. Each of the chosen creature’s Checks have Advantage
:
**Exalted (+1)** ::Each of the chosen creature’s Save Checks are also Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.


![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

## Evocations
Spells cast by invervention from extraplanar entities, or through the Primos' response to powerful emotion.

### Flame

#### Grasp of Aurodeus
*Flame Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Vowing a claim to victory, you grip your opponent with a flaming palm. Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can touch. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Thermic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Thermic damage equal to your level.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Thermic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Burning 1.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Ward of Gold
*Flame Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: 1 minute

Praising the day, you evoke a faint golden light. Choose up to 4 creatures you can sense within range, then roll your Mod Die and note the result. A ward protects you and those creatures.

For the duration, when damage would be dealt to you or one of the chosen creatures, prevent that damage, until you have prevented an amount of damage equal to your level plus 5 and the result of your roll. Once that amount has been prevented, the spell ends, and any remaining damage that would be dealt is dealt.

#### Blazing Lance
*Flame Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** ::Self, 15-space line
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands, a weapon
**Duration** :: Instant

With the might of a thousand swings, a blazing lance of fire erupts from your weapon. You Strike, and each creature in a 15-space line pointing from you in the direction of your target makes a $[PER] Save Check against a DC of 10 plus the value of your Mod Die for the Attack Check.

\column

**Failure** :: 9$[SL] Thermic Damage.
**Deadly Failure** :: 14$[SL] Thermic Damage.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (4th)** :: The Attack Check of your Strike is Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Attack Check of your Strike is Bolstered 2, instead of 1.
}}

#### Breath of Aurodeus
*Flame Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, $[SL] plus 2-space cone
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

With a deep breath, you stoke the fires of civilization and exhale a wave of fire. Each creature in a $[SL] plus 2-space cone makes an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 5$[SL] Thermic damage, Burning 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: 8$[SL] Thermic damage, Burning 2.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** ::  Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Burning 1 on a Success, and the Burning level on any Failure increases by 1. The size of the cone increases by 5 spaces.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Burning level on any result other than a Complete Success increases by 1.
}}

#### Flame of Ambition
*Flame Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Components** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Instant

Igniting the kindle within your heart, you burn with ambition. You take 10$[SL] plus 10 Thermic damage, then gain 20$[SL] plus 20 *Temporary Endurance*.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The *Temporary Endurance* you gain increases by 5.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The *Temporary Endurance* you gain increases by 15, instead of 5.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The *Temporary Endurance* you gain increases by 30, instead of 15.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Morning Glory
*Flame Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, $[SL] plus 2-space cone
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Raising your arms above your head, you call out to the dawn. Sparkling light flashes in a $[SL] plus 2-space cone, forcing each creature in the cone to make a $[PER] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 5$[SL] Thermic damage, the creature’s $[PER] Checks are Weakened until the end of your next turn.
**Deadly Failure** :: 8$[SL] Thermic damage,  Blinded until the end of your next turn.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Blinded on a Failure or a Deadly Failure. On a Success, its $[PER] Checks are Weakened until the end of your next turn.
**Exalted (5th)** :: A creature is Blinded on a Success as well as a Failure.
}}

#### Rekindle the Embers
*Flame Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Fanning the embers in your dying allies, you rekindle their battle fire. Choose $[SL] Allies you can sense within range that have 0 *Endurance*. Those creatures regain 1 *Endurance* and gain 7$[SL] plus 10 *Temporary Endurance*.

### Frost

#### Nightmother's Gaze
*Frost Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 3 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

As you praise the night for its chill, the air grows cold. Make an Attack Check against the $[VIG] of a creature you can sense within range. Add your Mod Die to the Arctic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Arctic damage equal to your level, Freezing 1.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Arctic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Freezing 2.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

\column

#### Silver Shade Thicket
*Frost Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces, 2-space circle
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Calling out to the wild, its wardens intervene. Choose a space you can sense within range. A 2-space circle centered on that point becomes Treacherous Terrain until the start of your turn as silver-cyan shadows of thick plants and small beasts hinder the movement of creatures within it.

#### Bestial Communion
*Frost Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Varies

Placing your hands on the ground, you let the rhythm of the wilds resonate through your voice. Choose **Speak** or **Summon**.
:
##### Speak
For 10 minutes, you can speak with and understand the communication of any Beast within range. Beasts are limited in their knowledge and capacity to understand and relay information as appropriate for their $[INT] and $[PRS]. Interacting with a socially with a Beast usually requires a $[PER] / $[PRS] Check.
:
{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: Checks you make to interact with a Beast during the spell are Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell lasts for 1 hour.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell lasts for 24 hours.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Checks you make to interact with a Beast during the spell are Empowered.
}}
:
##### Summon
You summon a spirit of the wild. Choose a Beast up to level 2$[SL]. A silver shade of that beast appears in an unoccupied space within range. It follows the rules described below.
:
- ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the Beast.
  
- ***$[AP] Maximum.*** Its $[AP] Maximum is 3.
  
- ***Allied.*** It can’t speak and doesn’t communicate, but has a supernatural understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required). It will follow any request to attack, defend, or follow something, but will ignore complex requests or requests that go against its nature.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

* ***Duration.*** It persists for 24 hours, or until you cast this option of the spell again.

* ***Initiative.*** If summoned during an encounter, it shares your Initiative and takes its turn after yours.

* ***Statistics.*** It uses the stat block of your chosen Beast, but is also an Eidolon, has Somatic Resistance 2$[SL], and is Immune to the Bleeding and Bruised Conditions.

:
If the spirit drops to 0 *Endurance*, it dissipates and the spell ends.
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: You summon a second Beast of your choosing.

#### Freezing Reproach
*Frost Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 1 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: 1 minute

With the guttural snarls of a predator, you prepare to strike. At any point during the spell, if a creature you can touch makes an Attack Check against you, you can spend 1 $[AP] as a Reaction to make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of the attacking creature. When you do, the spell ends. Add your Mod Die to the Arctic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: 8$[SL] Arctic damage, Freezing 1
**Deadly Success** :: 12$[SL] Arctic damage, Freezing 2
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (2nd)** :: The Attack Check is Bolstered 1.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The results of the Attack Check are now as follows.
:
**Success** :: 8$[SL] Arctic damage, Freezing $[SL] minus 1.
**Deadly Success** :: 12$[SL] Arctic damage, Freezing $[SL].
**Failure** :: Half as much damage as a Success, Freezing $[SL] minus 2.
**Complete Failure** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The Attack Check is Bolstered 2, instead of 1.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The Attack Check is Empowered.
}}

#### Nixoria's Embrace
*Frost Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 4 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 6-space cone
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Hands clasped and eyes closed, you sing a hymn of night to beckon blessed sleep. Each creature in a 6-space cone makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: The creature loses $[SL] plus 2 $[AP].
**Deadly Failure** :: The creature loses $[SL] plus 4 $[AP]. If they have no $[AP] remaining, they fall Unconscious.
**Success** :: The creature loses $[SL] plus 1 $[AP].
**Complete Success** :: The creature loses half $[SL] $[AP].
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The size of the cone increases to 10 spaces.
**Exalted (5th)** :: On a Failure, a creature falls Unconscious if they have no $[AP] remaining.
}}

#### Whirling Frost
*Frost Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** 3 :: $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 2-space emanation
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

With a deep breath, you release freezing air from your lungs that whirls around you. Each creature in a 2-space emanation makes a $[VIG] save against your DC Check. You can spend an additional 2 $[AP] to increase the size of the emanation by 1 and the Freezing level on a Deadly Failure by 2.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 6$[SL] plus 3 Arctic damage
**Deadly Failure** :: 9$[SL] plus 5 Arctic damage, Freezing 1.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The size of the emanation becomes 3.
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Freezing 1 on a Failure, or Freezing 2 on a Deadly Failure.
**Exalted (5th)** :: The size of the emanation becomes 6.
}}

### Pneuma

#### Eerie Occurrence
*Pneuma Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 5 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 2-space emanation
**Components** :: None
**Duration** :: 1 minute

With a silent musing for mischief, you invite spirits to play.  Choose one or more of the following effects, which occur in a 2-space emanation for the duration.
:
- ***Eyes.*** Your eyes glow a shade of magenta.
  
- ***Light.*** Any light and flames within the emanation are tinted magenta and fluctuate erratically.
  
- ***Voice.*** Spirits amplify your voice, making it three times as loud and distorting it with reverberation (your volume is normal outside the emanation).
 
- ***Whispers.*** Unintelligible whispers fill the emanation.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}

#### Id Rebuke
*Pneuma Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Touch
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Reciting a prayer for equilibrium, you rebuke violence. Make an Attack Check against the $[VIG] of a creature you can touch. Add your Mod Die to the Psychic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Your level plus 1 Psychic damage, Pacified 1 until the start of your turn.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Psychic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Pacified 2 until the start of your turn.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Haunting Assault
*Pneuma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces, 10-space line
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Letting wrath imbue your soul, you stir nearby spirits into a rage. Choose a space within range. You draw a line from that space up to 10 spaces long. The line doesn’t need to be straight. Each creature in that line makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
**Failure** :: 7$[SL] Psychic damage, Panicked 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: 11$[SL] Psychic damage, Panicked 2.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: A creature is Panicked 1 on a Success, and the Panicked level on any Failure increases by 1.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The maximum length of the line increases to 15 spaces, and you create a second line. Creatures in more than one line make only one Save Check.
**Exalted (5th)** :: You create a third line.
}}

#### Neuron of Pneumax
*Pneuma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

Pleading to the Prismos for understanding, a neuron of Pneuma Light connects your mind to another. Choose $[SL] creatures you can sense within range. Each of those creatures makes an $[INT] Save Check against your DC Check.

\column

Each creature that suffers a Failure becomes connected to you by the neuron. When you Sustain this spell, each connected creature takes 2$[SL] plus 2 Psychic damage. Your connection to a creature ends if either of you are ever more than 10 spaces outside the range of the spell, or the creature ends its turn with total cover from you.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 4$[SL] plus 4 Psychic damage, and you experience a vision of one of the creature’s memories from the last 24 hours most relevant to you, as determined by the PM.
**Deadly Failure** :: 6$[SL] plus 6 Psychic damage, Muddled 1 while connected, and the memory you view can be from the last week.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell’s Range becomes 10 spaces.
}}

#### Soul Separation
*Pneuma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 3 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

With a prayer for perspective, you force the soul of a creature from their body. Choose $[SL] creatures you can sense within range. Each of those creatures makes a $[PRS] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Stunned $[SL] plus 1, and until the creature is no longer Stunned it is Muddled and Dulled 1.
**Deadly Failure** :: Stunned $[SL] plus 2, and until the creature is no longer Stunned it is Muddled and Dulled 2.
**Success** :: Stunned $[SL].
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (2nd)** :: The range increases to 5 spaces.
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Stunned $[SL] minus 3 on a Complete Success.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Rather than choosing creatures, each Hostile creature in a 5-space emanation makes the Save Check.
}}

#### Spiritual Communion
*Pneuma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Varies

Bringing your emotions to the surface of your soul, you extend an invitation to the spirits around you. Choose **Speak** or **Summon**.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

##### Speak
For 10 minutes, you can speak with and understand the communication of any Ethereal within range, including those in the Soulscape. You can’t sense their presence, but you hear their thoughts, and they can hear yours.
:
{{left
**Exalted (+1)** :: Checks you make to interact with an Ethereal during the spell are Bolstered 1 for each exalted level.
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The spell lasts for 1 hour.
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell lasts for 24 hours.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Checks you make to interact with an Ethereal during the spell are Empowered.
}}
:
##### Summon
You summon a spirit from the Soulscape. Choose an Ethereal up to level 2$[SL]. That Ethereal appears in an unoccupied space within range. It follows the rules described below.
:
* ***PM Control.*** The PM controls the Ethereal.
  
* ***$[AP] Maximum.*** Its $[AP] Maximum is 3.

* ***Allied.*** It has a supernatural understanding of any verbal commands you give it (no $[AP] required). It will follow any request to attack, guard, or follow something, but will ignore complex requests or requests that go against its nature.
  
* ***Duration.*** It persists for 24 hours, or until you cast this option of the spell again.
  
* ***Initiative.*** If summoned during an encounter, it shares your Initiative and takes its turn after yours.

* ***Statistics.*** It uses the stat block of your chosen Ethereal.

:
If the spirit drops to 0 *Endurance*, it dissipates and the spell ends.
:
{{left
**Exalted (5th)** :: You summon a second Ethereal of your choosing.
}}

Letting your mind flare white with rage, you strike your foe with a clenched fist. Make an Attack Check against the AC of a creature you can touch. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Crushing damage dealt.

{{left

#### Feral Awakening
*Soma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

You release a feral roar, filling your allies with adrenaline. Choose up to 5 creatures you can sense within range. For the duration, those creature’s $[VIG], $[AGI], and $[PER] Save Checks are Bolstered 1.
:
{{left
**Exalted (4th)** :: The creature’s Save Checks are Bolstered 2, instead of 1.

#### Fleshmending Hymn
*Soma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You sing aloud the amplified noises of growing flesh and bone. Choose $[SL] creatures you can sense within range and make a Skill Check using your Spell Mod Die. For each creature, compare the Check Total against a DC of 10 plus the amount of *Vitality* that creature is missing.
:
{{left
**Success** :: The Bleeding, Bruised, and Poisoned Conditions end for that creature, and they regain 1 *Vitality*.
**Complete Success** :: As a Success, and the creature regains an additional *Vitality*.
**Failure** :: The Bleeding Condition ends for that creature, and its Poisoned level reduces by $[SL].
**Complete Failure** :: The Bleeding Condition ends for that creature.
:
**Exalted (4th)** :: The spell’s Range becomes 10 spaces.
}}

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

#### Trial of Plague
*Soma Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 3-space cone
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Cursing the feeble-bodied, you invoke a trial of sickness upon the weak. Each creature in a $[SL] plus 2-space cone makes a $[VIG] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
**Failure** :: Poisoned 2.
**Deadly Failure** :: Poisoned 3.
**Success** :: Poisoned 1.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.

:
{{left
**Exalted (4th)** :: The results of the Save Check are now as follows.

**Failure** :: Poisoned $[SL].
**Deadly Failure** :: Poisoned $[SL] plus 1.
**Success** :: Poisoned half $[SL].
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (5th)** :: You can increase the spell’s Cast Time to 4 $[AP]. If you do, the spell affects all Hostile creatures in a 6-space emanation instead of a cone.
}}

#### Wrath of Somaria
*Soma Evocation, 1st-level*
{{left
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces, cylinder (1-space-radius, 3 spaces high)
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant
}}

Balling your fury into a fist, you slam the ground, and from the Fleshcage a massive fist slams in kind. Choose a space you can sense within range. Each creature in a 1-space-radius, 3 space high cylinder centered on that point makes an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 5$[SL] plus 8 Crushing damage, Prone.
**Deadly Failure** :: 8$[SL] plus 12 Crushing damage, Prone, Stunned 1.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (+2)** :: The radius and height of the cylinder increases by 1 space.
**Exalted (4th)** :: Choose a second space you can sense within range at least 3 spaces away from the first. Another fist slams down, creating a second cylinder centered on that space.
}}

\column

### Mordant

#### Crushing Current
*Mordant Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

You spread your arms and force lesser creatures out of your path. Ocean water surrounds and propels you, allowing you to move up to your Class Bonus plus 4 spaces if you aren’t Immobilized. During this movement, you can pass through spaces occupied by creatures your Size or smaller. Any such creature whose space you pass through makes their choice of a $[VIG] or $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: Crushing damage equal to your level.
**Deadly Failure** :: Additional Crushing damage equal to your Class Bonus, Prone.
**Success** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Frothing Spittle
*Mordant Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

Letting your mind froth with disgust, you catch a  thousand curses in your throat and release them in a sling of spittle. Make an Attack Check against the $[AGI] of a creature you can sense within range. Add the value of your Mod Die to the Caustic damage dealt.
:
{{left
**Success** :: Caustic damage equal to your level, Dissolving 2.
**Deadly Success** :: Additional Caustic damage equal to your Class Bonus, Dissolving 3.
**Failure** :: Nothing.
}}

#### Caustic Curse
*Mordant Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

Filled with hatred, you curse the very existence of your wretched foe. Choose a creature you can sense within range. That creature makes a $[PER] Save Check against your DC Check in an attempt to ignore you. If the creature can’t hear you, the spell has no effect.

Each time you Sustain the spell, the chosen creature repeats the Save Check if they are within range. You can’t Sustain this spell if you can’t speak.

![](https://i.imgur.com/hd3lq4V.png){position:absolute,top:0,left:0,opacity:0.5,width:100%}
![](https://i.imgur.com/NQCPF0W.png){position:absolute,bottom:0.3%,left:0,width:100%}
{{pageNumber,auto}}

\page

**Failure** :: 5$[SL] plus 8 Caustic damage.
**Deadly Failure** :: 8$[SL] plus 12 Caustic damage.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
{{left
**Exalted (3rd)** :: You can choose $[SL] minus 1 creatures you can sense within range, instead of one.
**Exalted (5th)** :: A creature takes an additional 7 Caustic damage on a Failure, or 11 on a Deadly Failure.
}}

#### Jaws of Mordax
*Mordant Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: Self, 3-space line
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

With a wave of your hand, you reject everything in front of you. An ocean wave shaped into fearsome jaws is conjured from your space, extending in a 3-space line pointed in a direction of your choice. Each creature in the line or within 1 space of the line (excluding you) makes an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check.
:
{{left
**Failure** :: 7$[SL] plus 4 Crushing damage.
**Deadly Failure** :: 11$[SL] plus 6 Crushing damage, Dissolving 2, Prone.
**Success** :: Half as much damage as a Failure.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: The size of the wave increases to a 10-space line.
**Exalted (4th)** :: A creature is Dissolving 2 on a Failure, or 3 on a Deadly Failure.
**Exalted (5th)** :: Creatures within 2 spaces of the line also make the Save Check.
}}

#### Thalassic Plunder
*Mordant Evocation, 1st-level*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 10 spaces
**Components** :: Two Hands
**Duration** :: Instant

The greed in your soul is manifested, taking what is now yours. Choose $[SL] Small or Tiny objects you can sense within range. If there is an unobstructed path from the object to you, a current of ocean water is conjured around it that flows into your hands, bringing the object with it. If the object is being stowed on a creature, that creature can make an $[AGI] Save Check against your DC Check. If it is being held by them, they can make a $[VIG] Save Check instead.

\column

{{left
**Failure** :: The object flows into your hands uninterrupted.
**Deadly Failure** :: As a Failure, and the creature is Buffeted $[SL].
**Success** :: The current is interrupted and the object falls to the ground.
**Complete Success** :: Nothing.
:
**Exalted (3rd)** :: A creature is Buffeted half $[SL] on a Failure.
}}

### Lunar

#### Astral Projection
*Lunar Mantra*
**Cast Time** :: 3 $[AP]
**Range** :: 5 spaces
**Components** :: Spoken, Two Hands
**Duration** :: Sustained, 1 minute

Holding your arms above your head, you look beyond the sky to move without form. An illusory duplicate of yourself appears in an unoccupied space within range. For the duration, you control the duplicate with the rules below.

:

* ***Cosmic Insight.*** You have Advantage on $[INT] Checks for the duration.

* ***Illusory Mimicry.*** You take Actions and move as the duplicate. But any Action that would interact physically with the world or deal any damage except Cosmic has no effect outside of being a visible illusion.

* ***Sensory Relay.*** You experience nothing in your body, but sense from the duplicate as though it were you.

* ***Silent.*** The duplicate is completely silent and can’t speak or make noise. You may cast Lunar spells from the duplicate’s position, however.

:

The spoken requirement of this spell may be whispered, but you must be able to speak. When you cast this spell and each time you Sustain it, you become Morphing 1. This Condition ignores any Morphing Resistance you have.

Channeling Platinum Light through your hand, you restore health. Touch a non-Obluminous creature and make a Skill Check using your Spell Mod Die against a DC of 10 plus the amount of *Vitality* that creature is missing. Add your Mod Die to the *Endurance* regained.
:
{{left

{{wide

## Game Term Glossary & Formatting

}}

### Style Guide

Mechanical terms and Actions are Captialized. They are also **Bolded** the first time they appear.

"When you Strike, you make an Attack Check."
:
Resources are *Capitalized and Italicized*.

"You regain 5 *Endurance*."

### Glossary
**AP** :: $[AP] (Stands for Action Point) Resource used to perform actions, regained at the end of a creatures turn.
**Advantage** :: Modifies Checks by having the rolling entity roll an additional d20, taking the higher value between the d20s for the Check total.
**AGI** :: $[AGI] (Stands for Agility) Measure of how fast a creature can move.
**Ancestry** :: A PC's creature type chosen durning character creation.
**Arcane** :: Descriptor of mystical effects created with intentby sapient effort.
**Arcanum** :: Type of spell cast with personal talent.
**Attack Check** :: Check made when a creature attempts to harm something in a way that requires accuracy.
**Attribute** :: The measures of a creature's capability; Vigor, Agility, Perception, Intelligence, Presence.
**Attribute Check** :: Skill Check made to do something simple; abberviated as -Attribute- Check (e.g. $[PER] Check).
**Attribute Points** :: Measure of the potency of a given creature's attribute; abbreviated as $[VIG] Points, $[AGI] Points, etc.
**Basic Skill Check** :: Skill Check made to do something that requires basic knowledge of the task; always two Attributes rolled in Tandem. Abbreviated as "Tandem 'Attr.' / 'Attr.' Check", or simply "'Attr.' / 'Attr. Check'" (e.g. "Make a $[PER] / $[INT] Check").
**Bolstered X** :: Modifies Checks by increasing the size of the largest Mod Die by one step for each level (e.g., from a d6 to a d8).
**BtP** :: (Stands for Beyond the Prism) The name of this ttrpg.
**Check** :: The act of rolling a d20; includes Attack Checks, Save Checks, and Skill Checks.
**Check Total** :: The final result of a Check, compared against the DC.
**Class** :: Description of a PC's capabilities, gained through great dedication or natural talent.
**DC** :: (Stands for Difficulty Class) Number that a Check Total must meet or exceed for Success.
**DC Check** :: Special Check made by rolling a Mod Die and adding 10.
**Disadvantage** :: Modifies Checks by having the rolling entity roll an additional d20, taking the lower value between the d20s for the Check total.
**Empowered** :: Modifies Checks by either having a Tandem check add both Mod Dice to the Check total, or adding 1d4 to the Check total if one of the Mod Dice is a d2 or smaller.
**Endurance** :: Resource that represnts a creatures ability to function under high-stress situations. Damage is subtracted from Endurance.
**Evocation** :: Type of spell cast by borrowing power.
**Facet** :: A feature of a PC, comes from many sources.
**Hindered X** :: Modifies Checks by reducing the size of the largest Mod Die by one step for each level (e.g., from a d4 to a d2).
**Hobby** :: An activity a PC had experience in before they became an adventurer; allows a PC to make Trained Skill Checks related to them.
**INT** :: $[INT] (Stands for Intelligence) Measure of how smart a creature is.
**Maneuver** :: Technique requiring great physical or mental skill.
**Mod Die** :: (Stands for Modifer Die) A die rolled with a d20 when making a Check; often shorthanded to -Attribute- Die (e.g., $[VIG] Die).
**PER** :: $[PER] (Stands for Perception) Measure of how well a creature utilizes its senses.
**PC** :: (Stands for Player Character) A creature controlled by one of the players during a session.
**PM** :: (Stands for Prism Master) The game master; they control everything in the story apart from PCs.
**Profession** :: A PC's occupation before they became an adventurer; allows a PC to make money during Downtime and make Trained Skill Checks related to them.
**PRS** :: $[PRS] (Stands for Presence) Measure of how influential a creature is.
**Radiance** :: Resource that allows a creature to improve any Check they make by expending it.
**Save Check** :: Check made when a creature attempts to avoid something harmful.
**Skill Check** :: Check made when a creature tries to affect the world; often abbreviated as -Attribute(s)- Check (e.g. $[AGI] Check)
**Spell** :: Arcane or Supernatural Action with fantasic results.
**Supernatural** :: Descriptor of mystical effects created by extraplanar forces.
**Tandem** :: Term meaning to roll two Mod Dice together but only take the higher value for the Check total (e.g., $[VIG] / $[AGI] Check); shorthanded as " / ". Negative Mod Dice still subtract from the Check total.
**Trained Skill Check** :: Skill Check made to do something that requires specific training or knowledge; requires the appropriate Hobby or Profession. Abbreviated as "'Hobby or Profession' Check".
**VIG** :: $[VIG] (Stands for Vigor) Measure of a creature's health and strength.
**Vitality** :: Resource that represents a creautres physical wellbeing and how close they are to death; a creature that reaches 0 Vitality dies.
**Weakend** :: subtracting 1d4 from the Check Total.
